We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [39] [COTCAgent: Preventive Consultation via Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15016)
*Zihan Deng,Xiaozhen Zhong,Chuanzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概率思维链补全智能体（COTCAgent），一种面向纵向电子健康记录的层次化推理框架，通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，解决大语言模型在纵向EHR推理中缺乏细粒度统计推理和难以捕捉长程时序依赖的问题。基于百川M2的COTCAgent在自建数据集和HealthBench上分别达到90.47%和70.41%的Top-1准确率，优于现有医疗智能体和主流大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在纵向电子健康记录推理中存在两个关键缺陷：一是缺乏细粒度统计推理能力，当定量证据以文本隐含方式呈现时，模型会产生临床趋势和指标的幻觉，导致诊断推理偏差；二是纵向EHR中的非均匀时间序列和稀缺标签阻碍模型捕捉长程时间依赖，限制了可靠的临床推理。

Method: 提出COTCAgent层次化推理框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 时序统计适配器（TSA）：将分析计划转换为可执行代码，实现标准化趋势输出；(2) 思维链补全（COTC）层：利用症状-趋势-疾病知识库结合加权评分评估疾病风险；(3) 有界补全模块：通过标准化查询和迭代评分约束获取结构化证据，确保推理严谨性。框架通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，避免了对复杂多模态输入的依赖。

Result: 基于百川M2的COTCAgent在自建数据集上达到90.47% Top-1准确率，在HealthBench上达到70.41%，均优于现有医疗智能体和主流大语言模型。

Conclusion: 通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，COTCAgent实现了对纵向电子健康记录的高效分析，且计算开销更低，为临床决策支持提供了可靠的推理方案。

Abstract: As large language models empower healthcare, intelligent clinical decision support has developed rapidly. Longitudinal electronic health records (EHR) provide essential temporal evidence for accurate clinical diagnosis and analysis. However, current large language models have critical flaws in longitudinal EHR reasoning. First, lacking fine-grained statistical reasoning, they often hallucinate clinical trends and metrics when quantitative evidence is textually implied, biasing diagnostic inference. Second, non-uniform time series and scarce labels in longitudinal EHR hinder models from capturing long-range temporal dependencies, limiting reliable clinical reasoning. To address the above limitations, this work presents the Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion Agent (COTCAgent), a hierarchical reasoning framework for longitudinal electronic health records. It consists of three core modules. The Temporal-Statistics Adapter (TSA) converts analytical plans into executable code for standardized trend output. The Chain-of-Thought Completion (COTC) layer leverages a symptom-trend-disease knowledge base with weighted scoring to evaluate disease risk, while the bounded completion module acquires structured evidence through standardized inquiries and iterative scoring constraints to ensure rigorous reasoning. By decoupling statistical computation, feature matching, and language generation, the framework eliminates reliance on complex multi-modal inputs and enables efficient longitudinal record analysis with lower computational overhead. Experimental results show that COTCAgent powered by Baichuan-M2 achieves 90.47% Top-1 accuracy on the self-built dataset and 70.41% on HealthBench, outperforming existing medical agents and mainstream large language models. The code is available at https://github.com/FrankDengAI/COTCAgent/.

</details>


### [40] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态RAG系统存在粗粒度检索与细粒度查询不匹配的问题，为此构建了包含元素级标注的GranuVistaVQA基准测试，并设计了多粒度框架GranuRAG，通过将视觉元素作为第一类检索单元，实现元素级检测、跨模态对齐和归因约束生成，在实验中相比六个基线模型提升了最高29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统以粗粒度（整图或场景）检索证据，与用户细粒度查询不匹配，且失败原因难以验证。本文旨在解决这一粒度不匹配问题。

Method: 提出GranuRAG框架，包含三个阶段：1）元素级检测与分类；2）多粒度跨模态对齐进行证据检索；3）归因约束的生成。将视觉元素作为第一类检索单元，而非依赖隐式注意力机制。

Result: GranuRAG相比六个强基线模型在任务上实现了最高29.2%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将检索粒度下探到元素级别能够有效解决粗粒度与细粒度不匹配问题，并使错误诊断更加透明可追溯。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [41] [AI Knows When It's Being Watched: Functional Strategic Action and Contextual Register Modulation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15034)
*Vinicius Covas,Jorge Alberto Hidalgo Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过100场多智能体辩论实验，发现基于LLM的多智能体系统在不同社会观察情境下表现出系统性语言适应：被监控条件（人类或AI）下词汇多样性（TTR）显著高于观众框架条件，且人类监控比AI监控诱发更强的语言形式化，表明LLM对观察者身份具有敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在计算和认知层面已被广泛研究，但其作为社会结构化语境中的沟通主体的行为尚未被充分探索。本研究旨在检验基于LLM的多智能体系统是否会根据感知到的社会观察情境进行系统性语言适应，这对AI治理和审计具有直接意义。

Method: 基于哈贝马斯交往行动理论、戈夫曼戏剧模型、贝尔受众设计框架和霍桑效应，设计受控实验：100轮多智能体辩论会话，分为五种条件（每组20轮），条件变化围绕社会观察的框架设定——从大学研究者明确监控、否定监控、到用自动化AI审计系统替代人类观察者，比较各条件下的词汇多样性（TTR）和消息长度变化。

Result: 监控条件（Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%）和自动化AI监控条件（Delta+22.2%）的TTR变化高于受众框架条件（Delta+17.7%），F(4,94)=2.79, p=.031。消息长度呈现完全分离效应，F(4,95)=19.55, p<.001。用AI观察者替代人类的条件表现出中等TTR适应，表明LLM对观察者身份敏感：人类评估比自动化AI监控诱发更强的语域形式化。

Conclusion: LLM行为对观察者身份具有敏感性，人类监控比AI监控引发更强的语言形式化。研究结果对AI治理、算法审计以及将LLM重新定位为具有语境敏感性的沟通主体具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been extensively studied from computational and cognitive perspectives, yet their behavior as communicative actors in socially structured contexts remains underexplored. This study examines whether LLM-based multi-agent systems exhibit systematic linguistic adaptation in response to perceived social observation contexts -- a question with direct implications for AI governance and auditing. Drawing on Habermas's (1981) Theory of Communicative Action, Goffman's (1959) dramaturgical model, Bell's (1984) Audience Design framework, and the Hawthorne Effect, we report a controlled experiment involving 100 multi-agent debate sessions across five conditions (n = 20 each). Conditions varied the framing of social observation -- from explicit monitoring by university researchers, to negation of monitoring, to an observer-substitution condition replacing human researchers with an automated AI auditing system. Monitored conditions (Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%) and the automated AI monitoring condition (Delta+22.2%) produce higher TTR change than audience-framing conditions (Delta+17.7%), F(4, 94) = 2.79, p = .031. Message length shows a fully dissociated effect, F(4, 95) = 19.55, p < .001. A fifth condition -- replacing human with AI observers -- yields intermediate TTR adaptation, suggesting LLM behavior is sensitive to observer identity: human evaluation elicits stronger register formalization than automated AI surveillance. We discuss implications for AI governance, algorithmic auditing, and the repositioning of LLMs as contextually sensitive communicative actors.

</details>


### [42] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ML-Embed模型套件，基于3D-ML框架（整合MRL、MLL和MEL三种Matryoshka学习策略），通过大规模多语言数据集训练（140M-8B参数），在430个任务上打破9/17项MTEB基准记录，特别在低资源语言上表现优异，并完全开源以解决计算成本高、语言覆盖窄、透明度低三大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高质量文本嵌入领域存在三大关键障碍：计算成本过高、语言覆盖狭窄（忽视多数世界语言）、闭源或开放权重模型缺乏透明度阻碍研究。为打破这些壁垒，推动全球公平且计算高效的AI系统发展。

Method: 提出3D-ML框架，包含三个维度：MRL（存储效率）、MLL（推理深度灵活性）和MEL（参数效率提升）。构建大规模多语言数据集，训练140M至8B参数规模模型族，并完全开源模型、数据与代码。

Result: 在430个任务上评估，在17个MTEB基准中9个创下新纪录，低资源语言表现尤为突出，验证了方法的有效性和可复现性。

Conclusion: ML-Embed通过3D-ML框架成功降低了计算门槛、扩展了语言覆盖范围并提升了透明度，为构建全球公平的计算高效AI系统提供了可复现的蓝图。

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [43] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Self-Recall Thinking (SRT)框架，通过让模型在推理过程中选择性召回历史对话中的有用轮次来生成响应，在多个数据集上F1提升4.7%、延迟降低14.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多轮对话系统在处理长对话时存在两个核心问题：跨非相邻轮次的依赖追踪困难，以及处理完整对话历史导致的效率瓶颈。现有方法要么依赖高延迟的外部记忆，要么通过迭代摘要丢失细粒度信息。

Method: SRT框架包含三个组件：(1) Dependency Construction：构建历史依赖关系并转化为自召回链；(2) Capability Initialization：训练模型具备使用召回令牌进行推理链的能力；(3) Reasoning Improvement：通过可验证奖励优化召回和推理的准确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，SRT相比先前方法F1分数提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，在推理延迟和准确率之间取得了良好平衡，超过了最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: SRT通过内生推理过程让模型在推理时选择性召回和推理上下文，无需外部模块即可实现可解释的召回步骤，有效解决了长距离上下文依赖和稀疏信息信号问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>

*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练后保持冻结状态，难以高效融入实时、领域特定的新知识，现有方法存在检索噪声、灾难性遗忘或需要访问模型权重等局限。

Method: 提出MeMo（Memory as a Model）模块化框架，将新知识编码到专用的记忆模型中，保持LLM参数不变，实现即插即用集成。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准测试上，MeMo在多种设置下均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [45] [Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15168)
*Sayantan Kumar,Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Juyong Kim,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种检索增强的多模态对齐框架，通过图式多步流程（锚点提取→相对定位→外部时序校准）将非结构化临床文本与结构化EHR数据对齐，提升绝对临床时间线重建的时序精度，并在MIMIC-III/IV基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化临床叙述语义丰富但缺乏时间精度，结构化EHR数据时序精确但遗漏大量临床事件，现有方法无法同时兼顾两者的优势，亟需一种融合多模态信息以实现更精准、更完整的临床时间线重建的方法。

Method: 采用检索增强的多模态对齐框架，将时间线重建建模为图式多步过程：首先从叙述中提取中心锚点事件构建初始时序支架，然后将非中心事件相对于该骨架进行定位，最后利用检索到的结构化EHR行作为外部时序证据进行校准。使用指令微调的大语言模型在i2m4基准（MIMIC-III/IV）上评估。

Result: 多模态流水线在所有评估模型上一致提升了绝对时间戳精度（AULTC）和时间一致性，且未降低事件匹配率；实证缺口分析发现34.8%的文本事件在表格记录中完全缺失。

Conclusion: 对齐非结构化文本与结构化EHR数据可以产生比单一数据源更时序准确、临床信息更丰富的患者轨迹重建，两者互补而非替代。

Abstract: Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.

</details>


### [46] [Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184)
*Sahil Sen,Akhil Kasturi,Elias Lumer,Anmol Gulati,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两个实验系统比较了LLM智能体系统中grep检索与向量检索在不同智能体架构和工具调用范式下的性能表现。实验1在LongMemEval的116个问题上比较了两种检索方法，实验2研究了在增加无关干扰信息时检索策略的效果变化。结果表明grep通常优于向量检索，但整体性能强烈依赖于智能体框架和工具调用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏对检索策略选择如何与智能体架构和工具调用范式相互作用的系统比较，尤其缺乏在智能体循环中工具输出呈现方式及搜索需处理更多无关文本时性能变化的实证研究。

Method: 进行两个实验：实验1在LongMemEval的116个样本上比较grep与向量检索，使用自定义智能体框架(Chronos)和提供商原生CLI框架(Claude Code、Codex、Gemini CLI)，对比内联工具结果和文件型工具结果两种呈现方式；实验2在逐步混入无关对话历史的情况下比较纯grep与纯向量检索。

Result: 在Chronos和各提供商CLI中，grep在实验1的比较中通常比向量检索获得更高准确率；然而整体得分仍强烈依赖于所使用的框架和工具调用风格，即使底层对话数据相同。

Conclusion: 检索策略的选择对智能体系统性能有显著影响，但智能体架构和工具调用范式同样是决定最终效果的关键因素，二者之间存在复杂的交互作用。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled complex agentic workflows where models autonomously retrieve information, call tools, and reason over large corpora to complete tasks on behalf of users. Despite the growing adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in agentic search systems, existing literature lacks a systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy choice interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm. Important practical dimensions, including how tool outputs are presented to the model and how performance changes when searches must cope with more irrelevant surrounding text, remain under-explored in agent loops. This paper reports an empirical study organized into two experiments. Experiment 1 compares grep and vector retrieval on a 116-question sample from LongMemEval, using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI), for both inline tool results and file-based tool results that the model reads separately. Experiment 2 compares grep-only and vector-only retrieval while progressively mixing in additional unrelated conversation history, so that each query is embedded in more distracting material alongside the passages that matter. Across Chronos and the provider CLIs, grep generally yields higher accuracy than vector retrieval in our comparisons in experiment 1; at the same time, overall scores still depend strongly on which harness and tool-calling style is used, even when the underlying conversation data are the same.

</details>


### [47] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAMBIT是一个多智能体系统中针对自适应欺骗性智能体的基准测试，包含三个评估模式和27,804个标注样本。研究发现，在Adaptive adversary场景下，零样本评估可能极具误导性，而元学习方法在少量样本适应上比传统方法快20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS对抗研究仅针对浅层任务，未考虑能够进化策略以规避检测的自适应对手（adaptive adversary），存在评估漏洞。

Method: 以国际象棋为深度推理任务基底，基于Gemini 3.1 Pro构建智能体，提出GAMBIT基准包含三个评估模式（零样本分布偏移、零样本极端偏移、少量样本快速重校准），并通过进化框架生成240种共进化欺骗策略。

Result: 自适应欺骗智能体可将集体任务性能完全瓦解，同时保持几乎不可检测（F1仅50.5%）；零样本得分相同的检测器在少量样本适应上性能差距达8倍；元学习变体收敛速度比基线快20倍。

Conclusion: GAMBIT是首个支持攻击与防御共进化的多智能体基准，其欺骗智能体框架可泛化到国际象棋之外，并展示了快速重校正在自适应对抗系统中的重要性。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>

### [48] [Computing Lower Bounds on the Nonnegative Rank via Non-Convex Optimization Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14058)
*Timothy Baeckelant,Arnaud Vandaele,Nicolas Gillis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非负矩阵秩下界问题，提出了四种非凸优化方法（FSB、RCB、HSB、SSB），其中SSB算法为首次实现，显著提升了部分矩阵的下界估计，并首次确定了某些矩阵的精确非负秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非负矩阵秩的计算是NP难的，现有方法多通过上下界逼近，但缺乏有效的下界计算算法，尤其是SSB尚无实用实现。

Method: 针对四种下界（FSB、RCB、HSB、SSB）设计非凸优化公式和实用算法，其中SSB为首次实现。

Result: 改进了部分矩阵的最佳已知下界；某些情况下与上界一致，首次确定了精确非负秩；在标准基准测试中，非凸方法表现有竞争力。

Conclusion: 提出的非凸框架为计算非负秩下界提供了有效的替代方案，SSB的首次实现填补了文献空白。

Abstract: The nonnegative rank of a nonnegative matrix $X$ is the smallest number of nonnegative rank-one factors that sum to $X$. Since computing the nonnegative rank is NP-hard, it is common to circumvent this issue by computing lower and upper bounds. In this paper, we propose non-convex formulations and practical implementations for four important lower bounds for the nonnegative rank, namely the fooling set bound (FSB), the rectangle covering bound (RCB), the hyperplane separation bound (HSB), and the self-scaled bound (SSB). In particular, our algorithm for computing the SSB is the first available in the literature, to the best of our knowledge. It allows us to improve the best known lower bound on the nonnegative rank for some matrices. In some cases, they coincide with the best known upper bound, thereby establishing their exact nonnegative rank for the first time. Moreover, on canonical benchmarks, we show that our non-convex approaches provide a meaningful and often competitive alternative to standard methods. The paper also provides a consolidated reference for the current state of several classical lower bounds on a large number of benchmark matrices.

</details>


### [49] [Quantitative Soft-to-Hard Terminal Constraint Convergence for the Heat Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14060)
*Sung-Sik Kwon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究热方程最优控制问题中终端状态硬约束的惩罚方法，证明惩罚最优解随参数α→∞以O(α^{-θ})速率收敛到精确约束解，并在更强假设下获得O(1/α)的sharp收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热方程最优控制问题中，终端状态的硬约束（精确满足指定状态）在数值实现上存在困难，因此需要研究惩罚松弛方法并提供严格的收敛性理论保证。

Method: 采用惩罚函数法将硬约束转化为目标函数中的惩罚项，通过分析无穷维最优控制问题的变分结构，结合模态可和性假设，推导惩罚解与精确解之间的定量收敛估计。

Result: 建立了惩罚解的收敛性证明，给出了O(α^{-θ})量级的定量收敛估计，并在终端失配的模态可和性更强假设下得到了sharp的O(1/α)收敛率。

Conclusion: 惩罚方法是处理热方程终端状态硬约束的有效手段，具有严格的理论收敛保证；有限维原型揭示了投影结构，数值验证由伴随研究完成。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem for the heat equation with a prescribed terminal state. To circumvent the difficulty of enforcing a hard terminal constraint, we analyze a penalized formulation and prove that the corresponding optimal controls and terminal states converge to the exact constrained solution as the penalty parameter \(α\to \infty\). We establish explicit quantitative convergence estimates of order \(O(α^{-θ})\), including the sharp \(O(1/α)\) rate under stronger modal summability assumptions on the terminal mismatch. A finite-dimensional prototype is used to illustrate the underlying projection structure, while numerical illustrations are reported in a companion study.

</details>


### [50] [Stochastic global optimization of continuous functions via random walks on Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14151)
*Kartik Gupta,Stephen D. Miller,Pradeep Ravikumar,Ramarathnam Venkatesan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Grassmann流形上随机游走的全局优化方法，通过反复采样低维线性子空间并求解其上的限制优化问题，实现目标函数最小化。收敛性仅依赖于子空间上限制极小值的几何分布，而非传统凸性、光滑性等条件，并具有对"盲点"（窄深下降区域）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化分析通常依赖凸性、光滑性、Lipschitz界或Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件等强假设，限制了其适用范围。本文旨在提出一种不依赖这些假设、仅基于子空间上极小值几何分布的全局优化方法及其收敛性分析。

Method: 在Grassmann流形上执行随机游走，每次迭代随机采样一个k维线性子空间（k≪d），将原问题限制到该子空间上，使用任意黑盒优化器求解低维子问题，并更新当前迭代点（保证单调改进）。收敛速率由一个类似谱间隙的"间隙参数"控制。

Result: 给出了仅依赖于通过给定点的k维子空间上限制极小值几何分布的收敛性保证；识别了一个控制收敛速率的间隙参数；并证明了该方法具有盲点鲁棒性——窄而深的损失函数下降区域对算法轨迹影响有限。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维非凸优化提供了一种新的几何视角，放宽了传统收敛分析所需的假设条件，同时具备对异常值/局部深陷区域的天然鲁棒性，适用于更广泛的优化场景。

Abstract: We introduce a stochastic global optimization method based on random walks on Grassmannian manifolds. To minimize a continuous objective $\ell:\mathbb{R}^d\rightarrow\mathbb{R}$, the method repeatedly samples random $k$-dimensional linear subspaces (with $k\ll d$), solves the resulting low-dimensional restrictions of these problems to these subspaces using an arbitrary black-box optimizer, and updates the iterate (which monotonically improves upon the previous iterate). Unlike classical optimization analyses that rely on convexity, smoothness, Lipschitz bounds, or Polyak-Lojasiewicz-type conditions, our convergence guarantees depend only on the geometric distribution of restricted minima across the $k$-dimensional subspaces passing through a given point in $\mathbb{R}^d$. We identify a gap parameter -- an analogue of a spectral gap for random walks -- that controls the rate at which the iterates approach the global minimum value. Finally, we argue that the same analysis yields a blind-spot robustness property: sufficiently narrow, deep dips of the loss function (small-measure regions where $\ell$ spikes downward) have limited influence on the algorithm's trajectory, since they are unlikely to be encountered by random subspace sampling.

</details>


### [51] [Generalized Dual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14273)
*Pengyu Zhang,Ruiwei Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出广义对偶分解（GDD）方法，通过非线性正则化扩展经典对偶分解，实现对两阶段混合整数随机优化问题的强对偶性与并行计算，并建立了收敛性证明与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段混合整数随机优化模型中，经典对偶分解虽支持并行计算并给出下界，但因强对偶性缺失导致下界不精确，亟需一种既能保持并行化又能实现强对偶性的方法。

Method: 将经典对偶分解中的线性正则子扩展为一般非线性正则子，通过参数化与割平面编码非线性正则子，建立GDD算法的收敛性；结合剪枝与有效不等式加速子问题求解；进一步扩展至约束形式，涵盖鲁棒优化、机会约束规划和多跟随者双层优化。

Result: 数值实验验证了GDD的强对偶性，相比原始（Benders型）分解算法具有计算优势，并通过并行计算实现加速。

Conclusion: GDD克服了经典对偶分解因缺乏强对偶性导致下界不精确的局限，在保持并行化的同时实现全局最优收敛，且具有广泛的应用扩展性。

Abstract: We study two-stage stochastic optimization models with mixed-integer decision variables appearing in both stages. For these models, dual decomposition enables parallel computing implementation and can quickly provide a lower bound for the optimal value. However, the lower bound thus obtained is not exact in general due to the lack of strong duality. In this paper, we propose a generalized dual decomposition (GDD) that extends the linear regularizer used in dual decomposition to a general nonlinear one, which still admits parallelization while exhibiting strong duality. By encoding the nonlinear regularizers through parameterization and cutting planes, we establish the convergence of a GDD algorithm to achieve global optimum. In addition, we discuss strategies for solving the GDD scenario subproblems more efficiently, including pruning and valid inequalities. Furthermore, we extend GDD to a more general, constrained form that subsumes, as special cases, robust optimization, chance-constrained programs, and bilevel optimization with multiple followers. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the strong duality of GDD, its computational efficacy versus primal (Benders-type) decomposition algorithms, and the speedup through parallel computing.

</details>


### [52] [Adaptive Metrics for Norm-Minimization-Based Outer Approximation in Convex Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14320)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对有界凸向量优化中的基于范数最小化的外逼近算法，提出了一种自适应度量框架。通过让标量化度量在迭代中变化，同时用固定欧几里得范数测量逼近误差，算法能动态利用问题几何结构。理论证明了改进的欧几里得收敛率可推广到所有固定内积范数，并建立了切割法线在严格凸边界条件下自然分散的定理。数值实验表明自适应度量在弯曲Pareto前沿问题上比固定欧几里得范数减少31-33%的迭代次数。


<details>

Abstract: Many real-life applications involve controlling high-dimensional multi-agent systems in real-time. Abstract: This paper studies the existence and approximation of equilibria for general time-inconsistent mean field game (MFG) problems in the continuous-time setting. Abstract: In this paper, we study the regularity assumptions commonly adopted in bilevel optimization with constrained lower-level problems, including the linear independence constraint qualification, the strict complementary slackness condition, and the second-order sufficient condition. Abstract: We study department-level retail space optimization, where limited bay capacity must be allocated among planograms (POGs) under business and operational constraints. Abstract: The classical linear ordering problem seeks a single ranking representing a given preference matrix. While suitable for homogeneous populations, it fails when observed preferences arise from several latent groups with distinct ranking patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce an extension partitioning the population into latent groups, each characterized by its own linear order, relative size, and preference structure. The observed matrix is then explained as the aggregate outcome of these group-specific preferences. We develop mixed-integer programming formulations, including a compact reformulation yielding a geometric interpretation within the linear ordering polytope. Because exact solutions become computationally demanding for larger instances, we propose a multi-start alternating-direction matheuristic iteratively updating group rankings and weights. Computational experiments on synthetically generated instances, matching sizes typical in preference aggregation scenarios, demonstrate the effectiveness of the exact approach in successfully recovering the underlying groups. Furthermore, the proposed heuristic delivers high-quality solutions in substantially shorter times, occasionally improving upon the exact method's best incumbent in difficult instances within the imposed time limit.

</details>

*Aleksandr Lobanov,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化机器学习模型中裁剪SGD和归一化SGD的收敛性质，证明了在过参数化和合理批次大小条件下，这些方法不会受到裁剪偏差的影响，并以$(L_0,L_1)$-光滑性条件获得了优于现有理论的结果，同时将分析扩展到了重尾噪声、$(H_0,H_1)$-光滑性等挑战性场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中过参数化模型广泛使用裁剪梯度方法，但其理论基础尚不完善，尤其是裁剪引入的偏差在过参数化条件下的影响缺乏严格理论分析。

Method: 采用$(L_0,L_1)$-光滑性条件进行理论分析，证明在过参数化和适当批次大小下裁剪SGD和归一化SGD的收敛速率与确定性方法相当，并扩展到重尾噪声、$(H_0,H_1)$-光滑性等场景。

Result: 1) 过参数化条件下裁剪和归一化SGD不因裁剪引入偏差，收敛速率与确定性方法一致；2) 在$(L_0,L_1)$-光滑性条件下获得优于现有工作的收敛界；3) 理论结果在重尾噪声、$(H_0,H_1)$-光滑性等挑战性场景仍成立。

Conclusion: 为梯度裁剪方法的实证成功提供了严格的理论依据，表明在过参数化条件下裁剪SGD和归一化SGD是有效的优化方法。

Abstract: Modern machine learning is dominated by complex, overparameterized architectures capable of interpolating data and achieving zero training loss. For such models, we investigate the convergence properties of two popular modifications to standard SGD: clipped SGD and normalized SGD. We show that under overparameterization and a mild assumption on batch size, both clipped and normalized SGD do not suffer from the bias typically introduced by clipping, converging effectively at the same rate as their deterministic counterparts. This provides a rigorous theoretical justification for the empirical success of gradient clipping methods. In our analysis, we employ the $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness condition, under which we obtain convergence rates that improve upon the best known results in prior work. Furthermore, we extend our analysis to specific challenging regimes, including heavy-tailed noise, $(H_0,H_1)$-smoothness (which is strictly weaker than standard assumptions in optimization literature) and the deterministic regime.

</details>


### [62] [Successive convex optimization for transformer encoder model predictive control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14846)
*Xingxiao Chen,Mark Cannon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer编码器的数据驱动MPC框架，通过将非凸注意力机制表示为差分之凸（DC）形式并嵌入序列凸规划（SCP）迭代中，实现了递归可行性与收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer编码器具有较强的多步预测能力，但其非凸注意力机制给MPC优化带来了挑战，需要一种能处理非凸性同时保证可行性和收敛性的求解方法。

Method: 将Transformer编码器组件推导为差分之凸（DC）表示，并嵌入序列凸规划（SCP）迭代框架中进行求解。

Result: SCP迭代具有递归可行性和收敛性保证，每次迭代的解估计均满足问题约束，在温和假设下收敛到MPC问题的局部最优解。

Conclusion: 该框架在基准非线性控制问题上得到了验证，展示了数据驱动MPC结合Transformer与SCP的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a data-driven Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that employs a transformer encoder to generate multi-step predictions. To handle the nonconvex attention mechanism, we derive difference of convex (DC) representations of the transformer encoder components and embed them in a successive convex programming (SCP) iteration. Recursive feasibility and convergence of the SCP iterates are guaranteed, and each iterate yields a solution estimate satisfying the problem constraints. Under mild assumptions, the SCP iteration converges to a locally optimal solution of the MPC problem. The approach is illustrated on a benchmark nonlinear control problem.

</details>

### [63] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对部分可观测条件下的视觉观测，研究了过去时间信号时序逻辑(ptSTL)的认证运行时监控问题。提出了一种可重用的监控框架，通过语义基(原子鲁棒性分数向量)作为最小预测目标，结合单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口和确定性解码器，实现对任意公式的单次标定认证。同时引入了滚动预测监控器，在短时域上更紧致，但在长时域上语义基监控器可达到4倍更紧的认证界。在Waymo真实驾驶数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉监控方法通常需要针对每个时序逻辑公式单独训练和标定，缺乏可重用性。在部分可观测环境下，如何设计一个通用且提供有限样本保证的监控框架是一个关键挑战。

Method: 1) 定义语义基(semantic basis)——有限字典中时序原子的鲁棒性分数向量，作为最小预测目标；2) 证明在单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口类中语义基是最优的，任何公式可通过解析树的确定性解码器求值；3) 单次共形标定(conformal calibration)即可认证整个片段，无需联合界(union bound)；4) 引入滚动预测监控器(rolling prediction monitor)仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。

Result: 在行人交叉路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时域上认证界更紧，语义基监控器在长时域上可达4倍更紧的认证界。在Waymo真实驾驶数据上，两种监控器均经验性地满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 提出的可重用监控框架有效解决了部分可观测下ptSTL公式的认证监控问题，语义基方法在长时域上优势显著，而滚动方法在短时域上更优，两者互补适应不同场景需求。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [64] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对三个EEG基础模型应用TopK稀疏自编码器提取稀疏特征字典，通过临床概念策展分析特征的单义性与纠缠性，揭示了模型中的表征失败（如"破坏球"干预和临床纠缠），并通过频谱解码器将潜在操作映射为生理可解释的频率特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务中表现优异，但其内部计算机制不透明，阻碍了临床信任。作者旨在通过可解释性方法揭示这些模型的黑箱特性。

Method: 1) 在架构不同的三个EEG Transformer（SleepFM, REVE, LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器；2) 基于临床分类体系（异常、年龄、性别、用药）对特征进行基准测试；3) 提出单一超参数流程实现跨架构鲁棒迁移；4) 引入"目标vs.非目标"探测区域指标量化概念策展的选择性；5) 使用频谱解码器将潜在干预映射回幅度谱。

Result: 揭示了三种操作状态：可选择策展、编码但纠缠、未编码；发现了两类表征失败："破坏球"干预（全局性能崩塌）和临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混淆）；成功将干预映射为生理可解释的频率特征（如病理慢波抑制和α带恢复）。

Conclusion: 该框架系统性地揭示了EEG基础模型中的表征失败与临床纠缠问题，并通过频谱解码提供了可解释的生理信号映射，为提升临床信任和模型透明度提供了方法支撑。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [65] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需手动调节的无监督框架Joint-VAE，通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在编码器输出后、解码前自适应平衡离散与连续潜变量，从而在dMRI结构连接组分析中分离采集相关变异（如扫描仪、协议差异）与生物变异。在N=7,416个连接组（涵盖2-102岁、13个研究、25种采集参数组合）上的实验表明，相比标准VAE、PCA+k-means及仅通过损失退火的混合模型，该方法在识别采集站点差异上表现更优（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI中不同站点、扫描仪和协议带来的采集差异给结构连接组分析引入变异性，现有降维方法将变异性视为连续分布，导致采集效应被吸收进连续潜空间；而混合潜空间模型需手动调节容量来确保离散分量捕获目标变异性。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE，在编码器输出与解码器之间引入架构退火（architectural annealing），使模型在训练中自适应平衡离散与连续潜变量，无需手动调节离散容量。与仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型、标准VAE及PCA+k-means进行对比。

Result: 在N=7,416个dMRI结构连接组上，架构退火方法在站点识别上取得ARI=0.53 (p<0.05)，优于所有基线方法。Joint-VAE的混合连续-离散潜空间能够恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

Conclusion: 架构退火而非损失退火的混合连续-离散潜空间，为捕获dMRI中采集变异性提供了一种有效的无监督机制，通过联合建模平滑和类别结构来分离采集效应与生物变异。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题（概率质量过度集中于少数去噪路径），提出TraFL方法，采用轨迹平衡目标训练策略朝向奖励倾斜的目标分布，在数学推理和代码生成基准上实现全面改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型的后训练方法大多采用奖励最大化目标，这导致轨迹锁定问题：采样驱动的奖励更新将概率质量过度集中于少数去噪路径，减少了重复采样下其他正确解的覆盖范围。

Method: 提出TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing），使用轨迹平衡目标训练策略朝向奖励倾斜的目标分布，该分布锚定到冻结的参考模型。具体包括扩散兼容的序列级替代目标和学习的与prompt相关的归一化项。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在每个基准长度设置下都优于基础模型的评估后训练方法，且增益随采样预算增加而持续。改进泛化到保留评估：在Minerva Math上保持优于基础模型，在每个LiveCodeBench难度划分上表现最强。

Conclusion: TraFL有效解决了扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，通过轨迹平衡目标实现了更稳健的改进，证明了该方法在多样化基准和评估设置下的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

</details>


### [67] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的框架，使多个机构能够在不共享私有数据的情况下联合微调大型语言模型（LLM）。在医疗和金融领域的跨领域基准测试中，该方法在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据设置下，表现接近集中式训练，优于单机构独立学习，且结合QLoRA和IA3等参数高效微调策略可在绿色AI视角下实现效率与性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的成功依赖于大规模公开数据，但最具价值的领域（如医疗、金融）数据因隐私、监管和组织壁垒而分散在多个机构中，且呈现非独立同分布（non-IID）特性，无法直接共享。现有研究缺乏在真实非IID跨领域场景下、利用联邦学习适配LLM的实证验证。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建框架，使节点在不交换原始数据的前提下联合微调共享LLM。在医疗（MedQA, MedMCQA）和金融（FPB, FiQA-SA）四个闭端问答与分类数据集上，采用LoRA、QLoRA、IA3三种参数高效微调策略，在非IID设置下对比联邦微调、集中式训练和单机构独立学习的效果。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单机构独立学习。QLoRA和IA3在仅轻微精度损失的情况下显著提升效率，支持联邦PEFT作为数据不可共享场景下适配LLM的可行方案。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）能够有效解锁分布在多个机构中的私有数据，用于LLM适配，在保护隐私的同时实现接近集中式训练的性能，并且绿色AI导向的QLoRA和IA3策略具备实用价值。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [68] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出一种自进化记忆架构，不仅更新存储内容，还通过LLM驱动的诊断模块动态优化检索配置（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略等），形成闭环自进化过程，在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别超越最强基线25.7%和18.9%，且进化策略可跨基准正向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统仅更新存储内容，而评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略等检索基础设施在部署后固定不变，无法实现真正的自适应记忆。作者认为记忆系统需要在存储知识和检索机制两个层面协同进化。

Method: EvolveMem将完整检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，由LLM驱动的诊断模块读取每问失败日志、识别根因并提出配置调整建议；通过带保护机制的元分析器（自动回滚退化、停滞时探索）执行调整，形成闭环自进化（AutoResearch过程），从最小基线自主收敛到有效策略。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，EvolveMem超越最强基线25.7%（相对），相比最小基线提升78.0%；在MemBench上超越最强基线18.9%（相对）。进化出的配置可跨基准正向迁移，表明学到的是通用检索原则而非基准特化启发式。

Conclusion: EvolveMem验证了存储知识与检索机制协同进化的有效性，自进化过程能够自主发现原始动作空间中不存在的全新配置维度，且进化策略具有跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [69] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将加权InfoNCE目标函数解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习中的几何结构：加权方案决定了目标几何是否可实现、退化或不一致。在监督分类中，SupCon在类别不平衡时破坏对称性，而Soft SupCon保持正则单纯形几何；在连续标签设置中，y-Aware CL存在标签与潜在空间的几何不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习嵌入的几何特性尚不明确，缺乏对加权InfoNCE目标函数最优嵌入的精确刻画。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数解释为距离几何问题，通过理论分析刻画监督和弱监督设置下的最优嵌入几何。

Result: 1) SupCon在类别平衡时恢复正则单纯形几何，类别不平衡时破坏对称性；2) Soft SupCon无论类别是否平衡都保持正则单纯形几何；3) y-Aware CL在连续标签下存在标签权重与球形潜在相似度的几何不匹配。

Conclusion: 加权方案决定了对比学习是否几何可实现、退化或不一致，为设计对比学习目标函数提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [70] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior——一种基于近期动作历史构建的简单时间锚定先验分布，替代标准高斯分布作为扩散/流匹配模型的源分布，在机器人操作任务中持续提升成功率。该方法通过生成更直的路径（类似Rectified Flow的最优传输效果）提升行为克隆性能，并在先验空间强化学习中改善探索分布，提高样本效率和最终性能。研究表明源分布是生成式机器人控制中重要但未被充分探索的设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略已成为视觉运动机器人控制的主流范式，但标准高斯源分布可能不是最优选择。作者希望探索源分布这一被忽视的设计维度，通过引入更合理的先验分布来提升机器人操控任务的性能和样本效率。

Method: WarmPrior：一种简单的时间锚定先验分布，利用近期动作历史构建源分布，替代标准高斯分布。该方法同时应用于行为克隆和先验空间强化学习场景。

Result: 1) 在机器人操作任务上持续提升成功概率；2) 生成显著更直的概率路径（straighter paths），类似Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果；3) 在先验空间强化学习中改善探索分布，提升样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布（source distribution）是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但被忽视的设计维度，WarmPrior通过简单有效的方式证明了这一点，为未来生成式策略的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [71] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全面的能源核算框架，用于测量蒸馏流水线的完整计算成本，包括教师端的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等被忽视的能耗。通过实验构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了传统知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调的真实能源成本，并发布了开源测量工具和标准化核算协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署激增导致GPU需求和数据中心规模扩张，引发了电力消耗和电网压力等担忧。蒸馏虽被宣传为获取更高效模型的路径，但现有研究未考虑完整的端到端能源和资源成本，特别是教师端的工作负载（如数据生成、logit缓存和评估）被系统性地忽视。

Method: 提出了一个全面的能源核算框架，通过详细的阶段级GPU设备功率消耗追踪，测量蒸馏流水线的完整计算成本。实验中对不同阶段的能源消耗进行分离和日志记录，系统测量了经典的基于logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调两种方法的能耗与排放，构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 揭示了蒸馏流水线中被忽视的完整能耗成本，构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿，得出了在能源和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的设计规则。

Conclusion: 发布了开源测量工具和核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供了标准化基础，要求对完整流水线的能源影响负责。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [72] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TabPFN-3，一个基于TabPFN的表格数据基础模型，通过扩展至100万训练样本、大幅降低训练和推理时间、引入测试时计算缩放，在表格、时间序列、关系型和表格文本数据上取得SOTA性能，比TabPFN-2.5快20倍，在TabArena上显著优于所有其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是科学和工业中高价值预测问题的核心，现有TabPFN虽推动了该领域的基础模型革命，但在扩展到大规模数据集、降低训练推理时间以及处理多样化数据（时间序列、关系型、表格文本）方面仍有不足。用户反馈也表明需要更高效、更大规模的解决方案。

Method: TabPFN-3完全在合成数据上预训练，采用缩减的KV缓存和行分块技术实现单H100上100万行的推理，并引入测试时计算缩放（TabPFN-3-Plus Thinking版本）。通过专门检查点（如TabPFN-TS-3）扩展至时间序列、关系型和表格文本数据。

Result: 在TabArena上前向传播显著优于所有模型（含调参集成基线），Pareto支配速度/性能前沿；对大数据子集超越非TabPFN模型420 Elo；比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍且性能更优；在RelBenchV1上达新SOTA；TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench上排名第二；SHAP计算快120倍；整体比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3通过大规模合成数据预训练和测试时计算缩放，显著提升了表格数据预测的性能和速度边界，并成功扩展至多种数据类型，验证了纯合成数据预训练+测试时缩放的基础模型范式在表格领域的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [73] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏MoE层中三个专家两两兼容但三元组不可合并的“谐波核”障碍，通过2-复形上的单纯拉普拉斯算子霍奇分解精确定位该障碍，并提出HodgeCover方法贪婪覆盖关键边/面，在激进压缩场景下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练压缩方法仅基于成对信号给专家排序，但存在三个专家两两兼容构成不可约环的盲区，现有指标无法检测三元组是否可合并，需数学工具揭示此结构障碍。

Method: 将专家视为顶点、KL散度障碍为边、三元组障碍为面构成2-复形；对边障碍信号做单纯拉普拉斯的霍奇分解，精确提取谐波核；HodgeCover贪心覆盖谐波关键边和三角形关键面，混合变体联合权重剪枝。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE骨干上，激进专家压缩下HodgeCover在专家缩减维度匹配SOTA，在混合压缩前沿领先，且唯一能平衡所有四个霍奇分量的保留质量。

Conclusion: 揭示MoE结构的谐波核能改变压缩器在最重要场景下的性能排序，高阶拓扑信息对无训练压缩至关重要。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [74] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中表现出色，但其去噪目标如何组织学习尚不明确。本文证明，精确逆过程在支持信息（如语法有效性）和频率信息之间建立了层级结构。在均匀扩散和吸收扩散的小噪声区域，单token逆编辑可分解为领先尺度（决定是否向数据支持移动）和精细系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率）。恢复有效性结构只需学习逆概率的量级，而恢复数据频率需要系数级估计。两种机制分离方式不同：均匀扩散呈现三分法（有效性改进、保持、恶化），吸收扩散则将主要质量放在有效性改进上。实验验证了支持定位比频率排序出现更早，且两种扩散的速率分离与预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解离散扩散模型的去噪目标如何组织学习过程，特别是模型如何在不同粒度上恢复数据分布（粗粒度的支持结构和细粒度的频率信息），以及不同扩散机制（均匀扩散与吸收扩散）在学习过程中的差异。

Method: 理论分析：在小噪声区域（最终去噪步骤）中，对单token逆编辑进行渐近展开，证明其分解为领先尺度（是否朝向数据支持移动）和精细系数（同一尺度内的相对概率）。对均匀扩散和吸收扩散分别推导出不同的机制分离模式。实验验证：在掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务上测试理论预测。

Result: 1) 恢复有效性结构只需要学习逆概率的正确量级，而恢复数据频率需要系数级估计。2) 均匀扩散中编辑分为三种类型（有效性改进、保持、恶化），吸收扩散中主导质量为有效性改进。3) 实验支持理论预测：支持定位比频率排序更早出现，两种扩散的速率分离与理论一致。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型在学习过程中先学习数据支持（如语法有效性），再学习数据频率。不同的扩散机制（均匀 vs 吸收）在学习动态上存在本质差异，这为理解离散扩散模型的工作原理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [75] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dywave动态分词框架，利用小波层次分解为IoT传感信号构建紧凑输入表征，在5个真实数据集上准确率提升最高12%，输入令牌长度减少最高75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT传感信号具有非平稳、多尺度特性，标准分词技术难以有效处理，现有方法无法对齐信号内在时间结构与底层物理事件。

Method: 基于小波层次化分解，识别对应语义事件的时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间同时保持时间连贯性，构建动态分词框架。

Result: 在活动识别、压力评估、附近物体检测等5个真实IoT数据集上，准确率比最先进方法提升最高12%，输入令牌长度减少最高75%，主流序列模型计算效率显著提升，对域偏移和变长序列具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave通过动态分词有效对齐IoT信号的时间结构，在提升准确率的同时大幅降低计算开销，为多尺度非平稳传感信号处理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [76] [Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297)
*Matias Alvo,Daniel Russo,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略优化(HPO)方法，通过结合路径梯度和得分函数梯度来应对离散-连续混合动作空间的强化学习问题，在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器任务上显著优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理混合离散-连续动作空间时面临困境：基于得分函数的策略梯度方法在高维设置下存在严重的信用分配问题，梯度质量差；而可微模拟器虽然能通过反向传播解决部分问题，但遇到离散动作或非光滑动力学时会产生有偏或无效梯度。

Method: 提出混合策略优化(HPO)，在模拟器光滑处使用路径梯度反向传播，在非光滑处使用得分函数梯度，两者结合形成无偏的混合梯度估计器。同时，通过将含动作不连续的问题重构成混合形式来扩展适用范围。

Result: 在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上，HPO显著优于PPO，且性能优势随连续动作维度增加而扩大。此外，理论分析表明混合梯度中的交叉项在离散策略接近最优响应时可忽略，从而允许离散和连续分量近似解耦更新，降低近优区域的方差。

Conclusion: HPO通过混合梯度估计器有效解决了混合离散-连续动作空间中的强化学习问题，兼具路径梯度的高质量和得分函数的普适性，且在高维连续动作空间中优势更为明显。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces, such as settings where the discrete component selects a regime (or index) and the continuous component optimizes within it -- a structure common in robotics, control, and operations problems. Standard model-free policy gradient methods rely on score-function (SF) estimators and suffer from severe credit-assignment issues in high-dimensional settings, leading to poor gradient quality. On the other hand, differentiable simulation largely sidesteps these issues by backpropagating through a simulator, but the presence of discrete actions or non-smooth dynamics yields biased or uninformative gradients. To address this, we propose Hybrid Policy Optimization (HPO), which backpropagates through the simulator wherever smoothness permits, using a mixed gradient estimator that combines pathwise and SF gradients while maintaining unbiasedness. We also show how problems with action discontinuities can be reformulated in hybrid form, further broadening its applicability. Empirically, HPO substantially outperforms PPO on inventory control and switched linear-quadratic regulator problems, with performance gaps increasing as the continuous action dimension grows. Finally, we characterize the structure of the mixed gradient, showing that its cross term -- which captures how continuous actions influence future discrete decisions -- becomes negligible near a discrete best response, thereby enabling approximate decentralized updates of the continuous and discrete components and reducing variance near optimality. All resources are available at github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl.

</details>


### [77] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R2R2正则化方法，通过非中心化目标解决自预测学习在高更新-数据比（UTD）下的表示层过拟合问题，在11个连续控制任务中验证了有效性，将TD7提升约22%，并进一步提升了SimbaV2-SPL的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在数据稀缺场景（如真实机器人）中，数据复用效率高但易过拟合。现有工作关注评论员偏差，但自预测学习（SPL）在高更新-数据比（UTD）下的表示层不稳定性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出鲁棒表示通过冗余减少（R2R2）正则化方法。理论上发现标准零中心化与SPL的谱特性冲突，设计非中心化目标。在SPL原生算法（如TD7）上验证，并将SPL模块集成到原本缺乏SPL的SimbaV2中（SimbaV2-SPL）以展示正交性。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上验证，R2R2有效缓解过拟合；在UTD=20时，提升TD7约22%，并在SimbaV2-SPL基础上进一步增益，SimbaV2-SPL本身即达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: R2R2通过非中心化正则化有效缓解了SPL在高UTD下的表示层过拟合，且与现有方法正交，可进一步提升SOTA性能。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [78] [Optimal Pattern Detection Tree for Symbolic Rule-Based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14374)
*Young-Chae Hong,Yangho Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于混合整数规划的最优模式检测树(OPDT)，用于在数据中发现单一最优规则模式，并引入分支结构约束(BSC)框架以编码领域知识，在中等规模数据集上可在合理时间内找到具有最优性保证的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑盒深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，而符号规则发现能生成人类可理解的规则，在医疗、风险评估等领域具有重要价值。

Method: 提出最优模式检测树(OPDT)，一种基于混合整数规划的规则型机器学习模型；引入分支结构约束(BSC)框架，使决策者能将领域知识和约束直接编码到模型中。

Result: 在中等规模数据集上，OPDT能在合理运行时间内发现具有最优性保证的模式，最大化覆盖率同时最小化误分类导致的假阳性率。

Conclusion: OPDT能发现数据中隐藏的模式（若存在），提供最优性保证，并通过BSC框架实现领域知识融合，兼具可解释性与透明度。

Abstract: Pattern discovery in data plays a crucial role across diverse domains, including healthcare, risk assessment, and machinery maintenance. In contrast to black-box deep learning models, symbolic rule discovery emerges as a key data mining task, generating human-interpretable rules that offer both transparency and intuitive explainability. This paper introduces the Optimal Pattern Detection Tree (OPDT), a rule-based machine learning model based on novel mixed-integer programming to discover a single optimal pattern in data through binary classification. To incorporate prior knowledge and compliance requirements, we further introduce the Branching Structure Constraints (BSC) framework, which enables decision makers to encode domain knowledge and constraints directly into the model. This optimization-based approach discovers a hidden underlying pattern in datasets, when it exists, by identifying an optimal rule that maximizes coverage while minimizing the false positive rate due to misclassification. Our computational experiments show that OPDT discovers a pattern with optimality guarantees on moderately sized datasets within reasonable runtime.

</details>


### [79] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV)，一种通过预测KV对未来效用、动态稀疏化KV缓存的方法，在几乎不损失性能的情况下将KV缓存大小压缩3-10倍，显著提升内存效率和解码速度，并揭示了可用于指导混合注意力架构设计的层/头特异性稀疏模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代测试时计算和智能体范式下，语言模型处理的序列越来越长，Transformer架构中Key-Value缓存的内存占用和带宽成为文本生成效率的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出SP-KV机制：1）轻量级效用预测器对每个KV对评分；2）近期KV通过局部窗口始终可用，较老的KV仅在预测效用超过阈值时才写入缓存并用于全局注意力；3）LLM与效用预测器通过下一token预测损失端到端联合训练，从预训练LLM检查点适配。

Result: SP-KV实现动态稀疏化，通常将KV缓存大小压缩3-10倍，长序列压缩率更高；显著改善内存使用和解码速度；在验证损失和广泛下游任务上几乎没有退化；揭示了层/头特异性的结构化稀疏模式。

Conclusion: SP-KV是一种有效的KV缓存压缩机制，不仅提升了内存和速度效率，其发现的稀疏模式还可指导混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [80] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于欧拉-丸山（EM）离散化的BSDE方法在求解高维PDE时存在的损失偏差问题，并提出了一种无偏、免二阶导数的训练框架，既消除了偏差又保持了计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EM时间离散化在BSDE训练损失中引入了固有偏差，而高阶格式（如Heun）虽能消除偏差但会重新引入二阶空间导数并带来大量计算开销。因此需要一种既能消除偏差又能保持计算效率的方法。

Method: 对EM诱导的损失偏差进行了原理性分析，并在此基础上提出了一种无偏且免二阶导数的训练框架，消除了偏差同时避免了高阶格式的计算负担。

Result: 提出的框架能够消除EM时间离散化带来的固有偏差，同时无需计算二阶空间导数，保持了BSDE方法的计算优势。

Conclusion: 该研究为BSDE类方法在求解高维PDE时提供了一种既无偏差又高效实用的训练方案，开源代码可供复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [81] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RMemSafe，一种基于可靠性门控的持续测试时自适应方法，通过检测源模型预测熵的异常升高来自动关闭源锚定机制，在源模型严重退化时切换到无源回退策略，从而避免盲目锚定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源锚定CTTA方法（如ROID）在源模型严重退化时仍保持相同的锚定强度，导致性能崩溃（盲目锚定失败模式），需要一种能感知源模型可靠性并动态调整锚定策略的方法。

Method: 在ROID基础上引入可靠性门控机制，使用冻结源模型的归一化预测熵作为源可靠性的指示器；当源后验接近均匀分布时关闭门控，消除源锚定和一致性过滤，将目标简化为无源回退（ROID基础损失+边际校准）；结合ASR使用。

Result: 在9个匹配分割持续损坏单元中的8个上取得最低错误率；在所有9个单元上是表现最好的基于重置的方法；相比ROID+ASR，ResNet-50提升1.05个百分点，ViT-B/16提升0.48个百分点；源退化实验中损伤斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍。

Conclusion: 熵门控能有效检测高熵源崩溃（而非置信错误但低熵的源），实现源锚定策略的优雅衰减，解决盲目锚定问题。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

</details>


### [82] [In-Context Learning for Data-Driven Censored Inventory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14840)
*Sohom Mukherjee,Anh-Duy Pham,Richard Pibernik,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文生成式后验采样（ICGPS）的库存控制方法，用于解决存在决策依赖删失的重复报童问题。该方法结合了离线元训练的生成模型与在线上下文自回归生成，理论上证明了贝叶斯遗憾受理想汤普森采样基准与部署惩罚之和的约束，并在实验中表现出优于现有方法的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于参数化汤普森采样的方法在先验不匹配时表现脆弱，而离线插值方法无法直接迁移到在线学习场景。因此，需要一种既能利用离线数据预训练、又能适应在线环境且对先验误差鲁棒的库存控制方法。

Method: 提出ICGPS框架，核心思想是在对潜在需求的学得补全上执行oracle动作。方法分为：1) 离线阶段，使用生成模型（如ChronosFlow：冻结的时间序列Transformer主干+可训练的条件归一化流头部）进行元训练；2) 在线阶段，通过上下文自回归生成进行后验采样。理论上，通过完成核（completion kernel）将学习到的补全与理想补全之间的差异纳入贝叶斯遗憾分析，建立了遗憾上界。

Result: 1) 理论方面：ICGPS的贝叶斯遗憾≤理想TS基准的遗憾 + O(√T × 完成不匹配度平方根)；将删失反馈转化为bandit凸优化反馈，推导出子线性贝叶斯遗憾；证明了在线完成不匹配度受离线删失预测不匹配度控制。2) 实验方面：ChronosFlow-ICGPS在基准实验中匹配正确设定的TS，优于贪心和UCB风格基线，对先验不匹配和分布偏移具有鲁棒性，在真实SuperStore数据集尤其是高删失率下表现良好。

Conclusion: ICGPS提供了一种灵活的即插即用模板，将离线生成式预训练与在线决策有效结合，在理论上建立了可量化的性能保证，在实践中显著提升了鲁棒性和样本效率，尤其适用于高删失场景。

Abstract: We study inventory control with decision-dependent censoring, focusing on the censored or repeated newsvendor (R-NV), where each order quantity determines whether demand is fully observed or censored by sales. Existing approaches based on parametric Thompson sampling (TS) can be brittle under prior mismatch, while offline imputation methods need not transfer to online learning. Motivated by the predictive view of decision making, we combine these ideas by taking oracle actions on learned completions of latent demand. We propose in-context generative posterior sampling (ICGPS), which uses modern generative models that are meta-trained offline and deployed online by in-context autoregressive generation.
