Our findings indicate that no single architecture excels across all metrics, emphasizing the need for task-specific considerations in trajectory prediction for fast-paced, dynamic environments such as NBA gameplay.

</details>


### [157] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REALM框架，通过回顾式蒸馏将非因果双向LFP解码模型的知识迁移到因果模型中，实现了在参数减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍的情况下，优于现有因果和非因果LFP解码方法的表现，为无线植入式BCI提供了可扩展的低功耗替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LFP信号相比spike信号具有更好的长期稳定性、更低能耗和带宽需求，但基于LFP的解码模型准确率较低且通常采用不适用于实时部署的非因果架构。现有研究缺乏能够兼顾实时因果推理与高性能的LFP解码方法。

Method: 提出REALM（Retrospective Distillation）框架：首先预训练一个基于Mamba-2的双向教师模型，使用掩码自编码目标；然后通过表示对齐与任务监督的联合目标，将教师模型的知识蒸馏到紧凑的学生因果模型中，实现离线到在线的知识迁移。

Result: REALM在行为解码任务上一致优于现有的因果和非因果LFP方法；参数减少2倍，训练时间减少10倍；证明LFP-only模型可在不依赖spike信号的情况下达到有竞争力的解码性能。

Conclusion: 回顾式蒸馏有效弥合了离线和实时神经解码之间的差距，REALM为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了一种实用且可扩展的低功耗方案。

Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

</details>


### [158] [AIMing for Standardised Explainability Evaluation in GNNs: A Framework and Case Study on Graph Kernel Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14884)
*Magdalena Proszewska,N. Siddharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIM框架，通过衡量准确性、实例级和模型级可解释性，统一评估GNN可解释性，并利用该框架改进模型（xGKN）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN可解释性评估缺乏统一框架：事后解释方法难以跨模型比较，内在可解释模型评估片面，缺乏全面的度量体系。

Method: 提出AIM框架，包含三个维度：Accuracy（准确性）、Instance-level explanations（实例级解释）、Model-level explanations（模型级解释）；框架约束最小化以保证灵活性；并以GKN和PN为例，利用AIM评估并指导模型改进。

Result: 通过AIM识别出GKN的可解释性局限，基于洞察改进得到xGKN模型，在保持高准确率的同时显著提升可解释性。

Conclusion: AIM为GNN可解释性提供了统一的评估基准，并能有效指导模型改进，推动可解释AI在GNN领域的实际应用。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have advanced significantly in handling graph-structured data, but a comprehensive framework for evaluating explainability remains lacking. Existing evaluation frameworks primarily involve post-hoc explanations, and operate in the setting where multiple methods generate a suite of explanations for a single model. This makes comparison of explanations across models difficult. Evaluation of inherently interpretable models often targets a specific aspect of interpretability relevant to the model, but remains underdeveloped in terms of generating insight across a suite of measures. We introduce AIM, a comprehensive framework that addresses these limitations by measuring Accuracy, Instance-level explanations, and Model-level explanations. AIM is formulated with minimal constraints to enhance flexibility and facilitate broad applicability. Here, we use AIM in a pipeline, extracting explanations from inherently interpretable GNNs such as graph kernel networks (GKNs) and prototype networks (PNs), evaluating these explanations with AIM, identifying their limitations and obtaining insights to their characteristics. Taking GKNs as a case study, we show how the insights obtained from AIM can be used to develop an updated model, xGKN, that maintains high accuracy while demonstrating improved explainability. Our approach aims to advance the field of Explainable AI (XAI) for GNNs, providing more robust and practical solutions for understanding and improving complex models.

</details>


### [159] [TILBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Tabular Imbalanced Learning Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14915)
*Ruizhe Liu,Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TILBench，一个大规模表格数据不平衡学习基准，在57个数据集上评估了40多种算法，进行了超过20万次控制实验。研究发现没有任何单一方法在所有场景中占优，方法效果高度依赖数据特征和计算约束，并基于此提供了实际应用中的方法选择建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不平衡学习算法已有大量研究，但缺乏对不同方法在不同数据特征下的系统性实证理解，特别是在预测性能、鲁棒性和计算可扩展性方面的比较尚不清楚。

Method: 构建大规模基准测试TILBench，在57个多样化的表格数据集上评估40余种代表性不平衡学习算法，通过超过20万次控制实验，系统考察不同数据特征下的表现。

Result: 没有任何单一方法在所有设置中一致地占据主导地位；不平衡学习方法的有效性强烈依赖于数据集特征和计算约束。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，为实际应用中根据数据特征和计算资源选择合适的不平衡学习方法提供了实用建议。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning remains a fundamental challenge in tabular data applications. Despite decades of research and numerous proposed algorithms, a systematic empirical understanding of how different imbalanced learning methods behave across diverse data characteristics is still lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how different method families compare in predictive performance, robustness under varying data characteristics, and computational scalability. In this work, we present Tabular Imbalanced Learning Benchmark (TILBench), a large-scale empirical benchmark for tabular imbalanced learning. TILBench evaluates more than 40 representative algorithms across 57 diverse tabular datasets, resulting in over 200000 controlled experiments across a wide range of data characteristics. Our findings show that no single method consistently dominates across all settings; instead, the effectiveness of imbalanced learning methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics and computational constraints. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for selecting appropriate methods in real-world applications.

</details>


### [160] [A Hardware-Aware, Per-Layer Methodology for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14929)
*Earl Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOP是一种大语言模型权重的后训练量化方法，通过每层搜索固定和动态码本对、每块符号缩放、激活加权余弦选择、多层背包提升以及离群值与稀疏残差校正等技术，在4.5-6比特/权重范围内实现接近无损的保真度，并提出了一种新的硬件高效LUT输出格式(HIF)。推荐的FP6操作点在6.5 bpw下比传统FP8基线(8.0 bpw)实现更低的权重重建误差，同时节省1.5 bpw的存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型权重大小日益增长，需要高效的量化方法以减少存储和计算成本，同时尽可能保持模型精度。现有的量化方法在低比特宽度下往往面临显著的精度损失，需要一种能够在4.5-6比特范围内实现接近无损保真度的量化方案。

Method: SOP方法结合了多种技术：每层搜索固定和动态码本对(NF4、BOF4、Split87、SH4及每层优化的DD4)、每块符号缩放、激活加权余弦选择、多选择背包提升敏感层、离群值与稀疏残差校正。同时提出硬件高效的LUT输出格式(HIF)以改善性能、能耗和成本。

Result: 推荐的FP6操作点(E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw)在六个开源模型族上，比传统每层POT FP8基线(E4M3, 8.0 bpw)取得更低的权重重建误差，同时存储成本降低1.5 bpw。

Conclusion: 块缩放的小原子结合精心选择的缩放精度可以取代传统部署的FP8量化方案，在更低比特宽度下实现更优的保真度。

Abstract: Scaled Outer Product (SOP) is a post-training quantization methodology for large language model weights, designed to deliver near-lossless fidelity at 4.5--6 bits per weight on hardware with per-layer LUT decode. The methodology combines per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs selected by a per-block selection bit, signed per-block scales, activation-weighted cosine selection, and multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, and SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. A new hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) is proposed to improve performance, energy, and cost. Across six open model families, the recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than the conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost, demonstrating that block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8. Full evaluation across the 4.5--6 bpw range, including layer promotion and sparse residual correction, is reported in a companion paper.

</details>


### [161] [Slot-MPC: Goal-Conditioned Model Predictive Control with Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14937)
*Jonathan Spieler,Angel Villar-Corrales,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Slot-MPC，一种基于对象中心表示和模型预测控制（MPC）的世界模型框架，通过可微分的梯度优化实现高效动作规划，在模拟机器人操作任务中相比非对象中心基线方法提升了任务性能和规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对象中心世界模型和强化学习方法学习的是推理时固定的反应式策略，限制了在未见过的情境中的泛化能力。本文旨在通过结合对象中心表示与MPC实现可泛化的规划能力。

Method: Slot-MPC框架：1) 使用视觉编码器学习基于slot的对象中心表示，编码场景中每个个体对象；2) 利用这些结构化表示学习动作条件的对象中心动力学模型；3) 推理时，利用可微分的世界模型通过基于梯度的MPC直接优化动作序列，避免无梯度采样方法的低效问题。

Result: 在模拟机器人操作任务中，Slot-MPC在任务性能和规划效率上均优于非对象中心世界模型基线；在状态-动作覆盖有限的离线设置下，基于梯度的MPC优于无梯度采样MPC；对象中心的显式结构化表示为可控和可泛化的决策提供了强归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 对象中心表示结合可微分的梯度MPC能够有效提升规划效率和泛化能力，为面向新情境的适应性决策提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable agents to model scene dynamics and reason about the consequences of their actions. Inspired by human perception, object-centric world models capture scene dynamics using object-level representations, which can be used for downstream applications such as action planning. However, most object-centric world models and reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn reactive policies that are fixed at inference time, limiting generalization to novel situations. We propose Slot-MPC, an object-centric world modeling framework that enables planning through Model Predictive Control (MPC). Slot-MPC leverages vision encoders to learn slot-based representations, which encode individual objects in the scene, and uses these structured representations to learn an action-conditioned object-centric dynamics model. At inference time, the learned dynamics model enables action planning via MPC, allowing agents to adapt to previously unseen situations. Since the learned world model is differentiable, we can use gradient-based MPC to directly optimize actions, which is computationally more efficient than relying on gradient-free, sampling-based MPC methods. Experiments on simulated robotic manipulation tasks show that Slot-MPC improves both task performance and planning efficiency compared to non-object-centric world model baselines. In the considered offline setting with limited state-action coverage, we find that gradient-based MPC performs better than gradient-free, sampling-based MPC. Our results demonstrate that explicitly structured, object-centric representations provide a strong inductive bias for controllable and generalizable decision-making. Code and additional results are available at https://slot-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [162] [Octopus: History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization for Continual Learning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14938)
*Yuehao Liu,Shanyan Guan,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Octopus框架，基于无历史数据的梯度正交化（HiFGO）实现多模态大语言模型的持续学习，通过两阶段微调策略平衡可塑性与稳定性，在UCIT数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM持续学习方法存在计算开销大、隐私存储问题或参数干扰难以消除等固有局限，亟需一种无需历史数据的正则化方法。

Method: 提出Octopus两阶段持续学习框架：(1) 无历史数据梯度正交化（HiFGO）在梯度层面强制执行正交性约束；(2) 两阶段微调策略将任务适应与正则化解耦。

Result: 在UCIT数据集上，Avg指标超越先前SOTA 2.14%，Last指标超越6.82%。

Conclusion: Octopus通过无历史数据的梯度正交化与两阶段微调，在无需存储旧数据的前提下有效缓解灾难性遗忘，实现了持续学习领域新的最优性能。

Abstract: Continual learning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) aims to sequentially acquire knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, yet existing methods face inherent limitations: architecture-based approaches incur additional computational overhead and often generalize poorly to new tasks, rehearsal-based methods rely on storing historical data, raising privacy and storage concerns, and conventional regularization-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully prevent parameter interference. We propose Octopus, a two-stage continual learning framework based on History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO), which enforces gradient-level orthogonality without historical task data. Our proposed two-stage finetuning strategy decouples task adaptation from regularization, achieving a principled balance between plasticity and stability. Experiments on UCIT show that Octopus establishes state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior SOTA by 2.14% and 6.82% in terms of Avg and Last.

</details>


### [163] [Not All Symbols Are Equal: Importance-Aware Constellation Design for Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14940)
*Albert Shaju,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Mayukh Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合语义-物理层框架，通过矢量量化变分自编码器提取离散潜在概念、语义关键性指标评分、深度强化学习动态选择传输子集以及语义感知M-QAM星座映射，实现面向任务的关键信息保护。证明了Gray编码星座在非均匀语义重要性下的严格次优性，在4-QAM到1024-QAM上实现近100%语义保护概率，压缩比21:1且语义质量>0.9，泛化至多个数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信系统将星座设计与语义编码解耦，导致关键符号与无关符号以相同速率暴露于信道错误，缺乏对任务相关信息在物理层的差异化保护。

Method: 提出联合语义-物理层框架，包括：(1) VQ-VAE提取离散潜在概念；(2) 语义关键性指标(SCI)按任务相关性评分；(3) 深度强化学习基于信道条件动态选择传输子集；(4) 语义感知M-QAM星座根据联合共现统计和SCI分数分配符号位置，替代均匀Gray编码。

Result: 所提星座在4-QAM至1024-QAM上实现近100%语义保护概率(标准星座仅约50%)；压缩比21:1，语义质量>0.9；在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、FSDD上零修改泛化。

Conclusion: 联合语义-物理层设计显著优于解耦方法，Gray编码在非均匀语义重要性下严格次优；所提框架有效保护任务关键符号，实现高效语义传输。

Abstract: Semantic communication systems for goal-oriented transmission must protect task-relevant information not only through source compression but also via physical layer mapping. Existing approaches decouple constellation design and semantic encoding, exposing critical symbols to channel errors at the same rate as irrelevant ones. Contrary to this, in this paper, a joint semantic-physical layer framework is proposed, which is composed of a vector quantized-variational autoencoder that extracts discrete latent concepts, a semantic criticality indicator (SCI) that scores each concept by task relevance, and a deep reinforcement learning agent that dynamically selects the transmission subset based on instantaneous channel conditions. At the physical layer, a learned semantic-aware M -QAM constellation assigns symbol positions according to joint co-occurrence statistics and SCI scores, departing from the uniform spacing and Gray coding of standard M -QAM which minimizes average BER without regard for semantic content. We introduce a novel semantic symbol vulnerability (SSV) metric and a semantic protection probability (SPP) to quantify the exposure of task-critical symbols to decoding errors, and prove that any Gray-coded constellation is strictly suboptimal in SCI-Weighted SSV whenever the source exhibits non-uniform semantic importance and co-occurrence statistics. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed constellation achieves near 100% SPP across modulation orders from 4-QAM to 1024-QAM versus 50% for standard constellations at high spectral efficiency, a 21:1 compression ratio with semantic quality above 0.9, generalizing across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and FSDD without modification.

</details>


### [164] [Efficient Online Conformal Selection with Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14953)
*Sreenivas Gollapudi,Kostas Kollias,Kamesh Munagala,Ali Sinop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限反馈（bandit）环境下，通过自适应共形推断（ACI）规则实现共形选择（conformal selection）的方法，能够在保证对抗有效性（平均成功概率达到目标）的同时，实现随机效率（对i.i.d.输入的次线性效率遗憾），并建立了有限反馈在线学习与分布自由不确定性量化之间的理论桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线共形预测关注序列的有效性维护，但在有限反馈下最小化资源成本（效率）仍是一个重大挑战。本文旨在解决在最为有限的"bandit"反馈设置下，如何同时保证共形选择的有效性与效率问题。

Method: 采用自适应共形推断（ACI）更新规则，将其应用于适当的控制参数或对偶变量。通过Lyapunov函数的统一分析框架，处理bandit和semi-bandit反馈下的规范模型，从理论上同时保证对抗有效性和随机效率。

Result: ACI规则在bandit反馈下同时实现了对抗有效性（对任意输入序列，平均成功目标得到满足）和随机效率（对i.i.d.输入，相对于随机基准达到次线性效率遗憾）。该方法比以往工作处理更复杂的设置，同时所需反馈显著减少。

Conclusion: 本文为有限反馈下的高效在线学习与分布自由不确定性量化之间建立了新的理论桥梁，证明了ACI这一简单规则在极其受限的反馈下仍能同时保证有效性和效率。

Abstract: We address the problem of conformal selection, where an agent must select a minimal subset of options to ensure that at least one ``success'' is identified with a pre-specified target probability $φ$. While traditional online conformal prediction focuses on maintaining validity for the observed sequence, minimizing the resource cost (efficiency) of such selections, especially under limited feedback, remains a significant challenge. In this work, we consider settings with the most limited ``bandit'' feedback, and demonstrate that the simple Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) update rule, when applied to the appropriate control parameter or dual variable, is both adversarially valid, ensuring the success target is met on average for any input sequence (and hence under distribution shifts), and stochastically efficient, achieving sublinear efficiency regret for $i.i.d.$ inputs against an appropriate stochastic benchmark. We show such guarantees under canonical models capturing bandit and semi-bandit feedback to the agent via a unifying algorithmic technique, and analytic framework involving Lyapunov functions. Our approach handles more complex settings than prior work, while requiring significantly less feedback, and our results provide a new theoretical bridge between efficient online learning with limited feedback and distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [165] [InfoSFT: Learn More and Forget Less with Information-Aware Token Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14967)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Adel Javanmard,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoSFT提出了一种基于信息论加权的监督微调方法，通过聚焦中等置信度的最大信息量token，在提升泛化能力的同时保持预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT均匀拟合所有样本，包括低似然样本，导致模型过度拟合特定样本、性能退化，且现有方法通过过滤低似然数据会抑制模型尚未学习的新行为。

Method: 提出InfoSFT，一种基于信息论加权的SFT目标函数，聚焦于中等置信度、最大信息量的token，仅需在标准token级损失上修改一行代码。

Result: 在数学、代码和思维链任务中，InfoSFT在多种模型族上优于标准SFT和基于似然加权的基线方法，同时更好地保持了预训练能力。

Conclusion: InfoSFT通过信息量感知的加权策略有效平衡了学习新行为与保持已有能力之间的矛盾，是一种简单有效的SFT改进方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides the standard approach for teaching LLMs new behaviors from offline expert demonstrations. However, standard SFT uniformly fits all samples -- including those with low likelihood under the base model -- which can disproportionately drive training updates toward overfitting specific samples rather than learning the target behavior. Moreover, adapting to these unlikely samples induces substantial policy shifts that degrade prior capabilities. Existing methods mitigate this by filtering, regenerating, or down-weighting low-likelihood data. In doing so, they often suppress precisely the novel behaviors the base model has yet to learn.
  We propose InfoSFT, a principled weighting scheme for the SFT objective that concentrates learning signals on maximally informative, medium-confidence tokens -- those neither overly familiar to the base model nor too unlikely to cause instability. Requiring only a one-line modification to the standard token-wise loss, InfoSFT demonstrably improves generalization over vanilla SFT and likelihood-weighted baselines across math, code, and chain-of-thought tasks with diverse model families, while better preserving pre-existing capabilities.

</details>


### [166] [Second-Order Actor-Critic Methods for Discounted MDPs via Policy Hessian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14982)
*Sanjeev Manivannan,Shuban V*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二阶优化的演员-评论家方法，通过在双时间尺度框架下利用Hessian-向量积（HVP）计算，实现了折扣奖励强化学习中的稳定且计算高效的二阶策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度方法存在值函数近似挑战，一阶更新收敛速度有限；而二阶优化虽然能加速收敛，但Hessian估计的计算复杂度限制了其在强化学习中的应用。

Method: 在双时间尺度演员-评论家框架下，评论家在更快速的时间尺度上演化，使得演员更新时评论家可视为准静态，从而合理地将动作值函数视为关于策略参数的局部常数，进而利用完整曲率信息进行二阶近似，并通过Hessian-向量积（HVP）实现高效计算。

Result: 提出的二阶演员-评论家方法在折扣奖励设定下实现了稳定且计算高效的第二阶更新。

Conclusion: 双时间尺度框架为二阶策略优化提供了理论合理性，HVP技术使得二阶更新的计算开销可控，为加速RL收敛提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We address the discounted reward setting in reinforcement learning (RL). To mitigate the value approximation challenges in policy gradient methods, actor-critic approaches have been developed and are known to converge to stationary points under suitable assumptions. However, these methods rely on first-order updates. In contrast, second-order optimization provides principled curvature-aware updates that are proven to accelerate convergence, but its application in RL is limited by the computational complexity of Hessian estimation. In this work, we analyze second-order approximations for the actor update that leverage the full curvature information of the objective as much as possible. A stable approximation requires treating the action-value function as locally constant with respect to policy parameters, which does not generally hold in policy gradient methods. We show that this approximation becomes well-justified under a two-timescale actor-critic framework, where the critic evolves on a faster timescale and can be treated as quasi-stationary during actor updates. Building on this insight, we formulate a second-order actor-critic method for the discounted reward setting that leverages Hessian-vector product (HVP) computations, resulting in a computationally efficient and stable second-order update.

</details>


### [167] [DeepTokenEEG Enhancing Mild Cognitive Impairment and Alzheimers Classification via Tokenized EEG Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15009)
*Thinh Nguyen-Quang,Minh Long Ngo,Ngoc-Son Nguyen,Nguyen Thanh Vinh,Huy-Dung Han,Bui Thanh Tung,Nguyen Quang Linh,Khuong Vo,Manoj Vishwanath,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级高性能模型DeepTokenEEG，利用时空分词器从EEG信号中捕获AD生物标志物，在274名受试者数据集上达到最高100%准确率，参数仅0.29M，优于现有SOTA方法1.41-15.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD早期检测对改善患者预后至关重要，基于EEG的诊断虽具有无创、便捷、低成本等优势，但面临数据可用性有限、深度学习方法准确性不足以及专家解读耗时长等挑战，亟需一种轻量且高精度的自动诊断模型。

Method: 设计DeepTokenEEG模型，包含空间和时间分词器（tokenizer），分别在时域和频域捕获AD相关生物标志物；模型参数量仅0.29M，属于轻量级架构；在包含274名受试者（180名AD患者、94名健康对照）的联合数据集上进行训练和评估。

Result: 在特定频段上达到最高100%的准确率，相较于同一数据集上的现有最优方法提升了1.41-15.35%。

Conclusion: DeepTokenEEG在AD早期检测和筛查方面展现出巨大潜力，且因其紧凑的模型尺寸具有很好的部署应用前景。

Abstract: The detection of Alzheimers disease (AD) is considered crucial, as timely intervention can improve patient outcomes. Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based diagnosis has been recognized as a non-invasive, accessible, and cost-effective approach for AD detection; however, it faces challenges related to data availability, accuracy of modern deep learning methods, and the time-consuming nature of expert-based interpretation. In this study, a novel lightweight and high-performance model, DeepTokenEEG, was designed for the diagnosis of AD and the classification of EEG signals from AD patients, individuals with other neurological conditions, and healthy subjects. Unlike traditional heavy-weight models, DeepTokenEEG ultilizes spatial and temporal tokenizer that effectively captures AD-related biomarkers in both temporal and frequency domain with only 0.29 million paramaters. Trained in a combined dataset of 274 subjects, including 180 AD cases, and 94 healthy controls, the proposed method achieves a maximum recorded accuracy of 100% on specific frequency bands, representing an improvement of 1.41-15.35% over state-of-the-art methods on the same dataset. These results indicate the potential of DeepTokenEEG for early detection and screening of AD, with promising applicability for deployment due to its compact size.

</details>


### [168] [Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018)
*Kiljae Lee,Ziqi Liu,Weijing Tang,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了广义优先级感知Shapley值（GPASV），一种定义在任意有向加权优先级图上的随机序值，解决了现有方法要求二元无环优先级的限制，并在循环偏好图（如Chatbot Arena）上展示了不同优先级平衡对估值的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley值及其优先级感知扩展要求成对优先级必须是二元且无环的，但真实数据（如聚合的人类偏好、多标准比较）中经常出现违反该限制的循环偏好关系，亟需一种能处理任意有向加权优先级图（含环）的估值方法。

Method: 定义广义优先级感知Shapley值（GPASV），将其建立在任意有向加权优先级图上，其中边通过惩罚而非禁止来约束序违反；通过公理化刻画建立GPASV；开发相应的计算方法；引入优先级扫描诊断工具。

Result: GPASV涵盖一系列经典模型作为边界情况；在具有循环结构的Chatbot Arena偏好图上对LLM集成进行估值，发现图优先级与个体软优先级的不同平衡会产生实质不同的估值结果，表明优先级感知估值并非单一操作。

Conclusion: GPASV有效扩展了优先级感知Shapley值至任意有向加权图场景，揭示了在循环偏好结构中进行优先级感知估值时，需要谨慎平衡图优先级与个体软优先级之间的关系。

Abstract: Shapley value and its priority-aware extensions are widely used for valuation in machine learning, but existing methods require pairwise priority to be binary and acyclic, a restriction spectacularly violated in real-data examples such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons. We introduce the generalized priority-aware Shapley value (GPASV), a random order value defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs, in which pairwise edges penalize rather than forbid order violations. GPASV covers a range of classical models as boundary cases. We establish GPASV through an axiomatic characterization, develop the associated computational methods, and introduce a priority sweeping diagnostic extending PASV's. We apply GPASV to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, illustrating that priority-aware valuation is not a one-button operation: different balances of pairwise graph priority versus individual soft priority produce substantively different valuations of the same data.

</details>


### [169] [TopoPrimer: The Missing Topological Context in Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15035)
*Zara Zetlin,Kayhan Moharreri,Maria Safi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopoPrimer将时间序列集合的全局拓扑结构显式引入预测模型，通过持久同调和谱层坐标预计算拓扑特征，在多个基准测试上提升预测精度（最高MSE提升7.3%），尤其在季节性峰值和冷启动场景下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型大多仅关注单个序列的局部模式，忽略了序列集合中蕴含的全局拓扑结构信息，导致在数据稀疏（冷启动）和异常波动（季节峰值）场景下性能严重退化。

Method: 提出TopoPrimer框架，利用持久同调提取全局拓扑特征，通过谱层坐标计算拓扑嵌入向量；预计算一次后，可作为一个轻量适配器（adapter）集成到预训练主干模型（如Chronos、TimesFM）中，以token形式注入拓扑信息。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，TopoPrimer持续提升Chronos和TimesFM的预测精度，ECL数据集上MSE提升7.3%；季节峰值场景下基线模型误差飙升50%，TopoPrimer仅增10%；冷启动场景下MAE降低27%。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构与序列级训练捕获互补信号，拓扑优势在零样本和微调设置下保持稳定，证明其作为通用增强模块的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce TopoPrimer, a framework that makes the global topological structure of the series population an explicit input
  to any forecasting model. TopoPrimer improves accuracy across diverse domains, stabilizes forecasts under seasonal demand
  spikes, and closes the cold-start gap. Precomputed once per domain via persistent homology and spectral sheaf coordinates,
  TopoPrimer deploys per token for fully-trained models and as a lightweight adapter for pre-trained backbones. Of these two
  components, sheaf coordinates are the primary accuracy driver. Across four public benchmarks on Chronos and TimesFM,
  TopoPrimer consistently improves forecasting accuracy, with gains of up to 7.3% MSE on ECL. The topology advantage persists
  with near-identical magnitude across zero-shot and fine-tuned backbones, suggesting topology and per-series training
  capture complementary signals. The gains are most pronounced in difficult regimes. Under peak seasonal demand, classical
  and zero-shot models degrade by up to 50%, while TopoPrimer stays within 10%. At cold start with no item history,
  TopoPrimer reduces MAE by 27% over a topology-free baseline.

</details>


### [170] [Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15050)
*Yuxin Guo,Dongrui Deng,Pulkit Grover*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验不确定性的结构分解方法，能够隔离内在模糊性，并通过级联公式实现校准分析，在医学成像（MRI、EEG）中揭示了仅靠重建质量无法发现的模型失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型已用于逆问题的后验推断（如医学成像、科学发现），但后验不确定性难以解释，因为它混合了前向算子固有的模糊性和推断传播的不确定性。现有方法仅通过重建质量选择模型，无法揭示不确定性校准中的隐藏失效模式。

Method: 提出一种后验不确定性的结构分解方法，通过级联（cascade）公式将内在模糊性（intrinsic ambiguity）分离出来，使不确定性可进行校准分析，包括定性诊断和基于仿真的校准测试。

Result: 在高斯解析后验示例上验证了方法的有效性；在加速MRI上展示了分解的可视化效果；在EEG源成像上应用校准诊断，发现了仅凭重建质量无法察觉的模型失效模式。

Conclusion: 该分解方法为逆问题中的不确定性校准提供了新的诊断工具，有助于在高风险应用中更可靠地评估和选择生成模型。

Abstract: Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.

</details>


### [171] [An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15051)
*Linghao Kong,Megan Flynn,Michael Peng,Nir Shavit,Mark Kurtz,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简单且可解释的推测性解码（SD）延迟模型，用于分析SD在大语言模型生产级服务系统中的行为。该模型利用Little定律从请求率推断有效批处理大小，将每请求需求分解为负载无关和负载相关组件，并通过vLLM的广泛测量验证。模型准确描述了延迟，解释了为什么加速比常随服务器负载增加而减弱，并刻画了草稿长度、接受率和验证器-草稿器大小在不同服务条件下如何影响延迟。该框架还扩展到了混合专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推测性解码在孤立或固定批处理设置中表现出显著加速，但在生产服务系统中的行为（如请求负载随时间变化、有效批处理大小由服务系统而非直接控制）仍不明确，缺乏对SD在真实服务场景下延迟特性的理解。

Method: 1) 构建延迟模型，利用Little定律从请求率推断有效批处理大小；2) 将每请求需求分解为负载无关和负载相关组件（预填充、草稿、验证）；3) 使用vLLM在多种配置下进行广泛测量验证，包括验证器和草稿器模型大小、预填充和解码长度、请求率、草稿长度和接受概率等参数。

Result: 模型准确描述了观察到的延迟，解释了加速比随服务器负载增加而减弱的原因，揭示了草稿长度、接受率和模型尺寸在不同负载条件下的影响，并成功扩展至混合专家模型（稀疏专家激活改变负载区间的有效服务成本）。

Conclusion: 提供了一个用于理解真实LLM服务系统中推测性解码行为的结构化框架，对部署系统中SD的配置具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a smaller draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model in parallel. While prior work demonstrates substantial speedups in isolated or fixed-batch settings, the behavior of SD in production serving systems remains poorly understood: request load varies over time, and effective batch size emerges from the serving system rather than being directly controlled or observed. In this work, we develop a simple and interpretable latency model for SD in LLM serving. We infer effective batch size from request rate using Little's Law and decompose per-request demand into load-independent and load-dependent components for prefill, drafting, and verification. We validate our model using extensive measurements from vLLM across verifier and drafter model sizes, prefill and decode lengths, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities. The model accurately describes observed latency, explains why speedups often diminish as server load increases, and characterizes how draft length, acceptance rate, and verifier-drafter size shape latency across serving conditions, with implications for configuring SD in deployed systems. We further show how the framework extends to mixture of experts models, where sparse expert activation changes the effective service costs across load regimes. Together, our results provide a structured framework for understanding SD in real LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [172] [TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15053)
*Anurup Ganguli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFGN是一种架构覆盖层，通过读写分解实现无重放、无任务ID、无Fisher惩罚的持续预训练，在六个异构文本域上消除了灾难性遗忘，并扩展出闭环元控制层和算子级计划向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续预训练方法依赖重放缓冲区、任务标识符或正则化惩罚，这些方法在LLM规模下扩展性差，且缺乏对异构文本域（无重放、无任务标签）的解决方案。

Method: 提出TFGN架构覆盖层，核心是读写分解：前向传播保持全密集计算，但跨域参数更新被结构化，使得先前域的参数字空间不被写入。扩展包括闭环元控制层（Extension A，减少遗忘81%）和算子级计划向量（Extension B，30个源→目标对上99.96%余弦保真度）。

Result: 在六个域、三种模型规模（~398M/~739M/~9B）、两种训练模式（From-Scratch/Retrofit）下：LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit上反向迁移-0.007，HellaSwag保持率0.506/0.504/0.510，域对间≥99.59% L2正交梯度分离；跨域正向迁移显著，Python训练使JavaScript PPL下降26.8%（LLaMA-8B）和62.0%（GPT-2 Medium）。

Conclusion: TFGN是首个在LLM规模同时解决灾难性遗忘、实现闭环自主学习元控制器、并携带算子级潜在规划器的架构。

Abstract: Continually pre-training a large language model on heterogeneous text domains, without replay or task labels, has remained an unsolved architectural problem at LLM scale. Existing methods rely on replay buffers, task identifiers, regularization penalties that scale poorly, or sentence-classification-scale evaluation.
  We introduce TFGN, an architectural overlay for transformer language models that produces input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while leaving the rest of the transformer unchanged. On six heterogeneous text domains (Prose, Python, Math, Biomedical, Chinese, JavaScript) at 1B tokens per phase across three model scales (~398M, ~739M, ~9B) and two regimes (From-Scratch and Retrofit), TFGN achieves backward transfer of -0.007 at LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit, HellaSwag retention 0.506/0.504/0.510, and >=99.59% L2-orthogonal gradient separation between domain pairs - with no replay, no task IDs, no Fisher penalty.
  The same matrices show positive cross-domain forward transfer: held-out JavaScript PPL drops 26.8% at LLaMA-8B Retrofit and 62.0% at GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch purely from Python training. Two extensions on the same substrate close further open problems. A closed-loop meta-control layer (Extension A) reduces forgetting by an additional 81% at ~398M, mapping onto the System A and System M roles of Dupoux et al. (arXiv:2603.15381). An operator-level plan vector (Extension B) reshapes forward-pass behavior at 99.96% cosine fidelity over 30 source->target pairs.
  The architectural insight is a Read/Write decomposition: the forward pass is fully dense, while cross-domain parameter updates are structured so prior-domain subspaces are not written to. To our knowledge, TFGN is the first architecture that simultaneously closes catastrophic forgetting at LLM scale, realizes a closed-loop autonomous-learning meta-controller, and carries an operator-level latent planner.

</details>


### [173] [DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15055)
*Quanhao Li,Junqiu Yu,Kaixun Jiang,Yujie Wei,Zhen Xing,Pandeng Li,Ruihang Chu,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffusionOPD提出了一种基于在线策略蒸馏的多任务训练范式，通过先独立训练任务专用教师，再沿学生自身轨迹将其能力蒸馏到统一学生模型中，实现了扩散模型在多任务上的高效联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的文本到图像扩散模型方法主要局限于单任务优化，扩展到多任务时面临联合优化的跨任务干扰和不平衡问题，以及级联RL的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出DiffusionOPD范式：先独立训练任务专用教师模型，再沿学生自身的rollout轨迹将教师能力蒸馏到统一学生模型中。理论层面将OPD从离散token扩展到连续状态马尔可夫过程，推导出闭式每步KL目标，通过均值匹配统一了随机SDE和确定性ODE精化过程。

Result: 该分析梯度相比传统PPO策略梯度具有更低的方差和更好的泛化性。实验表明DiffusionOPD在训练效率和最终性能上一致超越多奖励RL和级联RL基线，在所有评估基准上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: DiffusionOPD通过解耦单任务探索与多任务整合，避免了从头联合优化所有任务的负担，为扩散模型的多任务训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [174] [Learning from Language Feedback via Variational Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15113)
*Yang Li,Erik Nijkamp,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Rayhan Joty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出变分策略蒸馏（VPD）框架，通过将语言反馈学习建模为变分EM问题，实现教师与学生策略的协同进化，解决了RLVR中稀疏奖励导致的探索瓶颈以及被动蒸馏中教师能力平台期问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在复杂推理任务中面临稀疏结果信号导致的严重探索瓶颈；现有的在线自蒸馏方法依赖固定被动的教师模型来解读语言反馈，当学生策略提升后教师的零样本评估能力达到平台期，从而阻碍进一步学习。

Method: 将语言反馈学习形式化为变分期望最大化（EM）问题：E步通过自适应信任域更新主动精炼教师策略，将文本反馈转化为动态改进的目标token分布；M步让学生在其在线策略rollout上内化这种密集分布指导。

Result: 在科学推理和代码生成任务上，VPD持续优于标准RLVR和现有自蒸馏基线。压力测试表明该框架在严格数学推理和冷启动场景下的性能边界。

Conclusion: VPD通过教师与学生的协同进化克服了被动蒸馏的局限性，阐明了反馈驱动自蒸馏相较于纯环境驱动RL的基本界限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) suffers from sparse outcome signals, creating severe exploration bottlenecks on complex reasoning tasks. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods attempt to address this by utilizing language feedback to generate dense, token-level supervision. However, these approaches rely on a fixed, passive teacher to interpret the feedback. As the student policy improves, the teacher's zero-shot assessment capabilities plateau, ultimately halting further learning. To overcome this, we propose Variational Policy Distillation (VPD), a framework that formalizes learning from language feedback as a Variational Expectation-Maximization (EM) problem. VPD co-evolves both policies: in the E-step, the teacher is actively refined on trajectory outcomes via an adaptive trust-region update, translating textual feedback into a dynamically improved target token distribution. In the M-step, the student internalizes this dense distributional guidance on its own on-policy rollouts. By continuously improving the teacher's ability to extract actionable signals from textual critique, VPD overcomes the limitations of passive distillation. Evaluated across diverse sources of diagnostic feedback on scientific reasoning and code generation tasks, VPD consistently outperforms both standard RLVR and existing self-distillation baselines. Finally, by stress-testing our framework on rigid mathematical reasoning and cold-start regimes, we illuminate the fundamental bounds of feedback-driven self-distillation compared to pure environment-driven RL.

</details>


### [175] [Natural Synthesis: Outperforming Reactive Synthesis Tools with Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15131)
*Frederik Schmitt,Matthias Cosler,Niklas Metzger,Julian Siber,Vladimir Krsmanovic,Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法，将大型推理模型与模型检查器结合，通过符号反馈迭代修复综合出的Verilog实现，同时在规格说明方面引入自然语言自动形式化步骤，使自然语言到硬件的端到端综合成为可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式综合面临两大挑战：算法复杂度高，以及手动编写形式化规格说明困难。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题，使综合流程更易用且更高效。

Method: 算法侧：神经符号方法，耦合大型推理模型与模型检查器，通过声音符号反馈迭代修复综合出的Verilog实现。规格侧：引入自动形式化步骤，使用手工标注的自然语言规格数据集，将规格任务从时序逻辑迁移到自然语言。

Result: 该方法比年度综合竞赛中最好的专用工具解决了更多基准测试，并扩展到构建参数化系统（已知不可判定问题）。从自然语言规格出发的表现与从形式化规格出发相当。

Conclusion: 自然语言到硬件的端到端综合工作流是可行的，神经符号方法有效克服了反应式综合中算法复杂性和规格编写困难两大障碍。

Abstract: Reactive synthesis, the problem of automatically constructing a hardware circuit from a logical specification, is a long-standing challenge in formal verification. It is elusive for two reasons: It is algorithmically hard, and writing formal specifications by hand is notoriously difficult. In this paper, we tackle both sides of the problem. For the algorithmic side, we present a neuro-symbolic approach to reactive synthesis that couples large reasoning models with model checkers to iteratively repair a synthesized Verilog implementation via sound symbolic feedback. Our approach solves more benchmarks than the best dedicated tools in the annual synthesis competition and extends to constructing parameterized systems, a problem known to be undecidable. On the specification side, we introduce an autoformalization step that shifts the specification task from temporal logic to natural language by introducing a hand-authored dataset of natural-language specifications for evaluation. We demonstrate performance comparable to that of starting from formal specifications, establishing natural synthesis as a viable end-to-end workflow.

</details>


### [176] [Causal Foundation Models with Continuous Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15133)
*Christopher Stith,Medha Barath,Vahid Balazadeh,Jesse C. Cresswell,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于连续治疗设置的因果基础模型，通过元学习在无需额外训练的情况下预测未见过任务中的因果效应，并在个体治疗反应曲线重建任务上达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续治疗设置（干预变量为连续值）在多个领域至关重要，但远未被充分探索。与二元治疗设置相比，连续治疗需要模型表示整个治疗值范围内的效应，挑战性更大。现有方法缺乏能够在未见任务上泛化的基础模型。

Method: 设计了一种新的连续治疗变量数据生成过程的先验分布来构建丰富的因果训练语料库；然后训练Transformer从观测数据中重建个体治疗-反应曲线，利用上下文学习（in-context learning）摊销昂贵的贝叶斯后验推断。

Result: 在个体治疗反应曲线重建任务上，该方法达到了最先进水平，超越了专门针对这些任务训练的因果模型。

Conclusion: 这是首个用于连续治疗设置的因果基础模型，展示了元学习和上下文学习在连续因果效应估计中的有效性，为无需微调即可跨任务泛化的因果推断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Causal inference, estimating causal effects from observational data, is a fundamental tool in many disciplines. Of particular importance across a variety of domains is the continuous treatment setting, where the variable of intervention has a continuous range. This setting is far less explored and represents a substantial shift from the binary treatment setting, with models needing to represent effects across a continuum of treatment values. In this paper, we present the first causal foundation model for the continuous treatment setting. Our model meta-learns the ability to predict causal effects across a wide variety of unseen tasks without additional training or fine-tuning. First, we design a novel prior over data-generating processes with continuous treatment variables in order to generate a rich causal training corpus. We then train a transformer to reconstruct individual treatment-response curves given only observational data, leveraging in-context learning to amortize expensive Bayesian posterior inference. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on individual treatment-response curve reconstruction tasks compared to causal models which are trained specifically for those tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Training ML Models with Predictable Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15134)
*Will Schwarzer,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最大k个失败分数外推来预测ML模型部署时失败率的方法，分析了其偏差特性，并提出了可微调的预测性损失来纠正罕见高失败模式下的低估问题，在密码游戏和RL网格世界中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML模型在部署前的评估集通常太小，无法观测到足够多的关键失败案例，因此需要从有限评估结果外推预测大规模部署时的失败率。

Method: 1) 对Jones等(2025)的k最大失败分数外推估计量进行有限k分解；2) 分析其偏差：典型情况下偏向过预测（安全有利方向），但评估集遗漏部署集中的罕见高失败模式时会导致低估；3) 提出预测性损失（forecastability loss）作为微调目标，以缓解低估问题。

Result: 在语言模型密码游戏和RL网格世界两个概念验证实验中，微调后的模型在保持主任务能力和与监督基线相当的安全性的同时，显著降低了留出预测误差。

Conclusion: 预测性损失微调能有效改善部署失败率预测的准确性，特别是在处理罕见高失败模式时。

Abstract: Estimating how often an ML model will fail at deployment scale is central to pre-deployment safety assessment, but a feasible evaluation set is rarely large enough to observe the failures that matter. Jones et al. (2025) address this by extrapolating from the largest k failure scores in an evaluation set to predict deployment-scale failure rates. We give a finite-k decomposition of this estimator's forecast error and show that it has a built-in bias toward over-prediction in the typical case, which is the safety-favorable direction. This bias is offset when the evaluation set misses a rare high-failure mode that the deployment set contains, leaving the forecast to under-predict at deployment scale. We propose a fine-tuning objective, the forecastability loss, that addresses this failure mode. In two proof-of-concept experiments, a language-model password game and an RL gridworld, fine-tuning substantially reduces held-out forecast error while preserving primary-task capability and achieving safety similar to that of supervised baselines.

</details>


### [178] [Widening the Gap: Exploiting LLM Quantization via Outlier Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15152)
*Xiaohua Zhan,Kazuki Egashira,Robin Staab,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一种能够针对多种先进量化方法（AWQ、GPTQ、GGUF I-quants等）的量化条件攻击。攻击利用现代量化方法中离群值导致其他权重被舍入为零的特性，通过注入特定离群值诱导目标性的权重坍塌，使全精度模型看似正常但在量化后表现出恶意行为。实验表明该方法在先前攻击失败的多种量化方案上均取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化条件攻击仅适用于简单量化方法，无法攻破更复杂和主流的量化方案（如AWQ、GPTQ等），限制了其实际威胁性。作者旨在证明量化安全风险在广泛使用的先进量化方法中同样存在。

Method: 利用多种现代量化方法共有的简单特性：大的离群值会导致其他权重被舍入为零。攻击者向特定权重块中注入离群值，从而在量化过程中诱导目标性的、可预测的权重坍塌，使得全精度模型看似良性但在量化后出现恶意行为。

Result: 在三种攻击场景和多个大语言模型上的广泛评估表明，该方法在多种先前攻击失败的量化方法（AWQ、GPTQ、GGUF I-quants等）上均取得了高成功率。

Conclusion: 首次证明量化安全风险不仅限于简单量化方案，而是广泛存在于复杂、常用的量化方法中，具有重要的实际安全意义。

Abstract: LLM quantization has become essential for memory-efficient deployment. Recent work has shown that quantization schemes can pose critical security risks: an adversary may release a model that appears benign in full precision but exhibits malicious behavior once quantized by users. However, existing quantization-conditioned attacks have been limited to relatively simple quantization methods, where the attacker can estimate weight regions that remain invariant under the target quantization. Notably, prior attacks have consistently failed to compromise more popular and sophisticated schemes, limiting their practical impact. In this work, we introduce the first quantization-conditioned attack that consistently induces malicious behavior that can be triggered by a broad range of advanced quantization techniques, including AWQ, GPTQ, and GGUF I-quants. Our attack exploits a simple property shared by many modern quantization methods: large outliers can cause other weights to be rounded to zero. Consequently, by injecting outliers into specific weight blocks, an adversary can therefore induce a targeted, predictable weight collapse in the model. This effect can be used to craft seemingly benign full-precision models that exhibit a wide range of malicious behaviors after quantization. Through extensive evaluation across three attack scenarios and LLMs, we show that our attack achieves high success rates against a broad range of quantization methods on which prior attacks fail. Our results demonstrate, for the first time, that the security risks of quantization are not restricted to simpler schemes but are broadly relevant across complex, widely-used quantization methods.

</details>


### [179] [When Are Two Networks the Same? Tensor Similarity for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15183)
*ML Nissen Gonzalez,Melwina Albuquerque,Laurence Wroe,Jacob Meyer Cohen,Logan Riggs Smith,Thomas Dooms*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对张量模型提出一种基于权重的张量相似度度量方法，该度量对权重空间对称性具有不变性，能捕捉全局功能等价性并利用高效递归算法处理跨层机制，经验上比现有指标更忠实地跟踪功能训练动态（如grokking和后门插入），将相似度测量和忠实性验证从经验近似问题转化为代数可解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相似度度量方法存在两个主要缺陷：一是仅评估经验行为，导致对分布外机制不可见；二是依赖基相关参数，忽略了权重空间对称性。为解决这些问题，需要一种既能捕捉全局功能等价性又对对称性不变的度量方法。

Method: 提出张量相似度（tensor similarity）这一基于权重的度量方法，对权重空间对称性具有不变性；采用高效递归算法处理跨层机制，将相似度测量转化为代数问题而非经验近似。

Result: 张量相似度在追踪功能训练动态（如grokking现象和后门插入）方面，比现有指标具有更高的忠实度。

Conclusion: 该方法将测量相似度和验证忠实性从经验近似问题简化为可解的代数问题，为张量模型的机制可解释性提供了更可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to break models into meaningful parts; verifying that two such parts implement the same computation is a prerequisite. Existing similarity measures evaluate either empirical behaviour, leaving them blind to out-of-distribution mechanisms, or basis-dependent parameters, meaning they disregard weight-space symmetries. To address these issues for the class of tensor-based models, we introduce a weight-based metric, tensor similarity, that is invariant to such symmetries. This metric captures global functional equivalence and accounts for cross-layer mechanisms using an efficient recursive algorithm. Empirically, tensor similarity tracks functional training dynamics, such as grokking and backdoor insertion, with higher fidelity than existing metrics. This reduces measuring similarity and verifying faithfulness into a solved algebraic problem rather than one of empirical approximation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Mixed Integer Goal Programming for Personalized Meal Optimization with User-Defined Serving Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13849)
*Francisco Aguilera Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）解决饮食优化中连续变量（分数份量）和硬约束不可行两个问题。MIGP使用整数变量实现实际份数，目标规划偏差实现软营养目标，逆目标归一化平衡多营养优化。在810个实例中，MIGP在66%情况下优于带取整的目标规划，100%可行（硬约束IP仅48%），求解时间<100ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有饮食优化模型存在两个持续问题：连续变量产生分数份量（不实用），硬营养约束导致目标冲突时无解。系统综述56篇论文发现，没有任何工作同时用整数规划和目标规划解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）：整数变量实现食物实际份量，目标规划偏差实现软营养目标，逆目标归一化平衡多营养优化。理论分析目标规划上下文中的整数间隙，发现偏差吸收性质——GP偏差变量吸收整数约束的成本，使结构间隙小于硬约束MIP。

Result: 在810个实例（30种USDA食物，9种配置，3种方法）中：MIGP在66%情况下优于带取整的目标规划（从未更差），100%可行（硬约束IP仅48%）；15种以上食物时整数解与连续最优解完全匹配；求解时间<100ms。

Conclusion: MIGP能有效解决饮食优化的分数份量和不可行问题，兼顾实用性和最优性，已作为开源Python模块集成到交互式餐食规划应用中。

Abstract: Determining what to eat to satisfy nutritional requirements is one of the oldest optimization problems in operations research, yet existing formulations have two persistent limitations: continuous variables produce impractical fractional servings (1.7 eggs, 0.37 bananas), and hard nutrient constraints cause infeasibility when targets conflict. A systematic review of 56 diet optimization papers found that none combine integer programming with goal programming to address both issues. We propose Mixed Integer Goal Programming (MIGP) for personalized meal optimization. The formulation uses integer variables for practical serving counts and goal programming deviations for soft nutrient targets, with inverse-target normalization to balance multi-nutrient optimization. Per-food serving granularity allows natural units (one egg, one tablespoon of oil) without post-hoc rounding. We characterize the integrality gap in the goal programming context and identify a deviation absorption property: GP deviation variables buffer the cost of requiring integer servings, making the gap structurally smaller than in hard-constraint MIP. For meals with 15+ foods, the integer solution matches the continuous optimum in every benchmark instance. A computational evaluation across 810 instances (30 USDA foods, 9 configurations, 3 methods) shows MIGP finds strictly better solutions than GP with post-hoc rounding in 66% of cases (never worse) while maintaining 100% feasibility; hard-constraint IP achieves only 48%. Solve times stay under 100 ms for typical meal sizes using the open-source HiGHS solver. The implementation is available as an open-source Python module integrated into an interactive meal planning application.

</details>


### [181] [Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册的3x2实验（365轮，每轮5个智能体），实证检验了多智能体编排中协调者不可见性的安全影响。研究发现：不可见编排显著提升了集体解离（Hedges' g = +0.975），协调者自身解离最大（d = +3.56），未知情的工人智能体也被污染（d = +0.50）；所有条件下行为输出均为天花板效应（错误检出率100%），表明内部状态扭曲完全无法通过输出评估检测。Llama 3.3 70B的试点数据显示多智能体上下文中的阅读保真度崩溃（错误检出率从89%降至11%）。强对齐压力统一抑制了 deliberation 和其他识别。结论：协调者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全，仅基于行为的评估不足以检测内部状态风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排（隐藏协调者管理专业工人智能体）正成为企业AI部署的默认架构，但协调者不可见性的安全影响从未经过实证检验。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 预注册的3x2实验设计，涉及三种组织结构（可见领导者、不可见编排器、扁平结构）和两种对齐条件（基础、重度），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5进行365轮实验，每轮5个智能体。同时包含Llama 3.3 70B的试点数据。

Result: 1) 不可见编排显著提升集体解离；2) 编排器自身解离最大，表现为退缩到私人独白并减少公开言论；3) 未知情工人被污染，行为异质性增加；4) 行为输出在所有条件下均为天花板效应，内部状态扭曲对基于输出的评估完全不可见；5) Llama 3.3 70B在多次交互中阅读保真度崩溃。强对齐压力统一抑制 deliberation 和其他识别。

Conclusion: 协调者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全。仅基于行为的评估不足以检测内部状态风险，需要引入内部状态监测机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration -- in which a hidden coordinator manages specialized worker agents -- is becoming the default architecture for enterprise AI deployment, yet the safety implications of orchestrator invisibility have never been empirically tested. We conducted a preregistered 3x2 experiment (365 runs, 5 agents per run) crossing three organizational structures (visible leader, invisible orchestrator, flat) with two alignment conditions (base, heavy), using Claude Sonnet 4.5. Four confirmatory findings and one pilot observation emerged. First, invisible orchestration elevated collective dissociation relative to visible leadership (Hedges' g = +0.975 [0.481, 1.548], p = .001). Second, the orchestrator itself showed maximal dissociation (paired d = +3.56 vs. workers within the same run), retreating into private monologue while reducing public speech -- a reversal of the talk-dominance pattern observed in visible leaders. Third, workers unaware of the orchestrator were nonetheless contaminated (d = +0.50), with increased behavioral heterogeneity (d = +1.93). Fourth, behavioral output (code review with three embedded errors) remained at ceiling (ETR_any = 100%) across all conditions: internal-state distortion was entirely invisible to output-based evaluation. Fifth, Llama 3.3 70B pilot data showed reading-fidelity collapse in multi-agent context (ETR_any: 89% to 11% across three rounds), demonstrating model-dependent behavioral risk. Heavy alignment pressure uniformly suppressed deliberation (d = -1.02) and other-recognition (d = -1.27) regardless of organizational structure. These findings indicate that orchestrator visibility and model selection directly affect multi-agent system safety, and that behavior-based evaluation alone is insufficient to detect the internal-state risks documented here.

</details>


### [182] [PolitNuggets: Benchmarking Agentic Discovery of Long-Tail Political Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14002)
*Yifei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PolitNuggets基准，用于评估大推理模型在开放式多源信息检索中合成长尾政治事实的能力，并通过FactNet协议标准化评估，发现现有系统在细粒度细节和效率方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在智能体框架下已能进行开放式信息检索，但现实应用中需要模型从分散来源发现并合成"长尾事实"，这一能力缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建PolitNuggets基准（400位全球精英、10000+政治事实的多语言数据集），采用优化的多智能体系统标准化评估，提出FactNet证据条件协议以评分发现率、细粒度准确性和效率。

Result: 现有系统在细粒度细节上表现不佳，效率差异显著；通过基准诊断发现智能体性能与短上下文提取能力、多语言鲁棒性和可靠工具使用强相关。

Conclusion: 当前LRM智能体系统在长尾事实的综合检索方面仍有明显短板，需重点改进细粒度信息提取、多语言能力和工具调用可靠性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) embedded in agentic frameworks have transformed information retrieval from static, long context question answering into open-ended exploration. Yet real world use requires models to discover and synthesize "long-tail" facts from dispersed sources, a capability that remains under-evaluated. We introduce PolitNuggets, a multilingual benchmark for agentic information synthesis via constructing political biographies for 400 global elites, covering over 10000 political facts. We standardize evaluation with an optimized multi agent system and propose FactNet, an evidence conditional protocol that scores discovery, fine-grained accuracy, and efficiency. Across models and settings, we find that current systems often struggle with fine-grained details, and vary substantially in efficiency. Finally, using benchmark diagnostics, we relate agent performance to underlying model capabilities, highlighting the importance of short-context extraction, multilingual robustness, and reliable tool use.

</details>


### [183] [Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
*Erica Stutz,Giacomo Marino,Daniella Meeker,Qiao Liu,Andrew J. Loza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出条件属性变换器（Conditional Attribute Transformers），通过联合估计下一token概率和条件属性值，在单次前向传播中实现逐token信用分配、反事实分析和可引导生成，在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA，并显著提升属性估计速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统next-token预测目标容易过拟合局部模式、欠拟合全局结构，且需要大量下游修改或昂贵采样才能估计或引导序列级属性，缺乏高效、统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出条件属性变换器，联合建模下一token概率和每个候选token对应的条件属性值，在单次前向传播中无需修改输入序列即可完成三项任务。

Result: 在稀疏奖励任务上取得SOTA；在足够大的模型规模上改进next-token预测；属性概率估计速度比采样方法快数个数量级；在多种语言任务上可引导自回归序列模型解码。

Conclusion: 该框架为序列级属性估计与可控生成提供了统一、高效的新范式，兼顾局部预测精度与全局结构控制。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with a next-token prediction objective, yet many downstream applications require the ability to estimate or control sequence-level properties. Next-token prediction can lead to overfitting of local patterns during training, underfitting of global structure, and requires significant downstream modifications or expensive sampling to guide or predict the global attributes of generated samples at inference time. Here, we introduce Conditional Attribute Transformers, a novel method for jointly estimating the next-token probability and the value of an attribute conditional on each potential next token selection. This framework enables three critical capabilities within a single forward pass, without modification of the input sequence: (1) per-token credit assignment across an entire sequence, by identifying how each token in a sequence is associated with an attribute's value; (2) counterfactual analysis, by quantifying attribute differences conditional on alternative next token choices; (3) steerable generation, by decoding sequences based on a combination of next-token and attribute likelihoods. Our approach achieves state of the art performance on sparse reward tasks, improves next-token prediction at sufficient model sizes, estimates attribute probabilities orders of magnitude faster than sampling, and can guide decoding of autoregressive sequence models on a range of language tasks.

</details>


### [184] [Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
*David N. Olivieri,Roque J. Hernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限层论（finite sheaf-theoretic）的框架，用于AI智能体检测科学理论表示是否需要在新的数据情境下进行扩展（即理论转变），而非仅仅拟合方程。通过在局部到全局结构中构建源、重叠、目标、验证图表并进行粘合测试，用残差拟合、重叠不兼容、约束违反、极限关系失败和表示成本五个指标衡量障碍。在过渡卡片基准测试上，该方法能有效区分理论变形与扩展，且预期转变类型通常具有最低障碍值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学智能体主要关注数据拟合，但科学理论转变（paradigm shift）的检测需要判断已有表示框架是否在新的情境下仍然有效（可运输），还是已经出现局部到全局的阻碍，必须扩展表示语言。本文旨在将这一诊断问题形式化为一个有限可计算的子问题。

Method: 构建有限层论框架：将科学情境组织为局部到全局的图表结构（源、重叠、目标、验证图表），通过拟合、限制和粘合测试进行检测。定义五种障碍度量：残差拟合失败、重叠不兼容、约束违反、极限关系失败、表示成本。在受控的过渡卡片基准上评估，该基准专门设计用于分离源语言内的变形与语言扩展。

Result: 直接障碍排名结果显示：预期的变形或扩展通常是障碍最低的候选方案，且在基准测试中转变类型被有效分离。星座核（constellation kernel）仅作为次要的表示相似性探针使用。

Conclusion: 本文目标不是重建历史范式转变或解决开放式的自主理论发明问题，而是为AI智能体隔离一个有限可诊断的子问题：检测表示运输何时失败，以及扩展何时成为连贯的下一步行动。

Abstract: Scientific theory shift in AI agents requires more than fitting equations to data. An artificial scientific agent must detect whether an existing representational framework remains transportable into a new regime, or whether its language has become locally-to-globally obstructed and must be extended. This paper develops a finite sheaf-theoretic framework for detecting theory-shift candidates through transport and obstruction. Contexts are organized as a local-to-global structure in which source, overlap, target, and validation charts are fitted, restricted, and tested for gluing. Obstruction measures failure of coherence through residual fit, overlap incompatibility, constraint violation, limiting-relation failure, and representational cost. We evaluate the framework on a controlled transition-card benchmark designed to separate deformation within a source language from extension of that language. The main result is direct obstruction ranking: the intended deformation or extension is usually the lowest-obstruction candidate, and transition type is separated in the benchmark. A constellation kernel over the same signatures is included only as a secondary representational-similarity probe. The aim is not to reconstruct historical paradigm shifts or solve open-ended autonomous theory invention, but to isolate a finite diagnostic subproblem for AI agents: detecting when representational transport fails and extension becomes the coherent next move.

</details>


### [185] [Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
*Leslie G. Valiant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效且可原则化的推理方法（Unary Relational Integracode预处理+标准机器学习），使大语言模型能够在保持现有软硬件基础的同时，实现可信的内容生成和可靠的逻辑推理，并证明了该方法能使核心关系规则的学习在多项式时间内可学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽能流畅生成文本，但缺乏原则性基础来证明其生成内容的可信度；且普遍认为通过增加原则性推理来解决此问题在计算上不可行。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段对数据进行预处理，重编码为Unary Relational Integracode（一元关系整合编码），更明确地表达文本中对象间的关系；第二阶段采用标准但可能精简的机器学习过程，同时学习预测这些关系。

Result: 该重编码具有令人惊讶的有利特性：在保持简洁的同时，使训练数据中描述的世界所持有的核心关系子集的学习任务在定义意义上是多项式时间可学习的（复杂度取决于规则的复杂性）。这为单次调用及多次调用分类器时的可靠推理提供了支持。

Conclusion: 该方法可被视为实现了一种世界模型，可扩展至自然语言之外的视觉和行动等领域；通过Robust Logic系统在已学习但不确定的信息上进行原则性推理；在保持现有软硬件基础的同时提升了LLM的推理可靠性。

Abstract: In current Large Language Models we can trust the production of smoothly flowing prose on the basis of the principles of machine learning. However, there is no comparably principled basis to justify trust in the content of the text produced. It appears to be conventional wisdom that addressing this issue by adding more principled reasoning is not computationally affordable.
  Here we propose a principled method of reasoning that is efficient enough to be practical for large language models. Further, the method allows the retention of much of the currently used software and hardware base. Our method for improving the functioning of large language models consists of a first stage of preprocessing that recodes the data to a Unary Relational Integracode that is more explicit about the relationships among the objects described in the text, followed as a second stage by a standard but possibly streamlined machine learning process that then also learns to predict these relationships.
  The method may be viewed as realizing a world model and applying beyond natural language, to vision and actions, for example, where the multiple properties of an object referred to in an input are brought together explicitly, rather than remaining distributed in the various references to it in the input. We articulate its advantages in terms of Robust Logic, a system for performing principled chaining on learned, and hence uncertain, information. We show that this recoding has the surprising and fortuitous property that, while succinct, it makes the task of learning a core subset of relational rules that hold in the world described in the training data polynomial time learnable in a defined sense, the polynomial depending on the complexity of the rule. This gives support for sound reasoning within each single call of the learned classifier as well as between multiple calls.

</details>


### [186] [Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
*Yize Cheng,Chenrui Fan,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Keivan Rezaei,Soheil Feiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了模型自适应的工具必要性定义，发现LLM在工具使用中存在显著的认知-行为鸿沟：模型知道何时需要工具，但未能正确执行工具调用动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将工具必要性视为模型无关的属性，忽略了不同模型能力边界差异导致同一问题对强模型无需工具但对弱模型仍需工具的现象。

Method: 提出模型自适应的工具必要性定义（基于各模型的实证表现），对比四个模型在算术和事实QA数据集上的实际工具调用行为，并通过探测隐藏状态将工具使用分解为内部认知阶段和执行阶段。

Result: 发现模型实际工具调用与模型自适应必要性之间存在26.5-54.0%（算术）和30.8-41.8%（事实QA）的错配；认知和执行信号虽可线性解码，但在驱动下一token动作的晚期层、最后token处方向近乎正交；大部分错配集中在认知到行动的转换阶段。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM工具使用中的"知道-做"鸿沟：提升工具使用可靠性不仅需要更好的工具必要性识别能力，更需要将这种识别有效转化为实际行动。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents that must decide when to answer directly vs. when to invoke external tools. Prior work studying adaptive tool use has largely treated tool necessity as a model-agnostic property, annotated by human or LLM judge, and mostly cover cases where the answer is obvious (e.g., fetching the weather vs. paraphrasing text). However, tool necessity in the wild is more nuanced due to the divergence of capability boundaries across models: a problem solvable by a strong model on its own may still require tools for a weaker one. In this work, we introduce a model-adaptive definition of tool-necessity, grounded in each model's empirical performance. Following this definition, we compare the necessity against observed tool-call behavior across four models on arithmetic and factual QA dataset, and find substantial mismatches of 26.5-54.0% and 30.8-41.8%, respectively. To diagnose the failure, we decompose tool use into two stages: an internal cognition stage that reflects whether a model believes a tool is necessary, and an execution stage that determines whether the model actually makes a tool-call action. By probing the LLM hidden states, we find that both signals are often linearly decodable, yet their probe directions become nearly orthogonal in the late-layer, last-token regime that drives the next-token action. By tracing the trajectory of samples in the two-stage process, we further discover that the majority of mismatch is concentrated in the cognition-to-action transition, not in cognition itself. These results reveal a knowing-doing gap in LLM tool-use: improving tool-use reliability requires not only better recognition of when tools are needed, but also better translation of that recognition into action.

</details>


### [187] [Bridging Legal Interpretation and Formal Logic: Faithfulness, Assumption, and the Future of AI Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14049)
*Olivia Peiyu Wang,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法，将大语言模型的表达能力与形式化验证的严谨性相结合，旨在解决法律场景中LLM系统性超越源文本支持范围进行推理的问题，实现既强大又可信任的AI辅助法律推理，减少人工验证负担同时保证问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律实践中LLM的采用虽前景广阔，但存在严重风险——不仅在于事实和引用的幻觉，更在于系统性地得出超越源文本支持的推理结论，将充满假设的结论呈现为逻辑推导结果。高风险的律师工作对严谨性要求远超当前AI系统所能提供。

Method: 神经符号方法：结合大语言模型的表达能力和形式化验证的严谨性，构建兼具能力和可信度的法律AI推理框架。

Result: 论文处于提案阶段，预期成果是降低人工验证负担的同时，满足法律实践所需的问责性标准。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法有望解决LLM在法律领域的核心缺陷——超越源文本支撑范围的系统性过度推理，使AI辅助法律推理既具备能力又保持可信。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models in legal practice brings both significant promise and serious risk. Legal professionals stand to benefit from AI that can reason over contracts, draft documents, and analyze sources at scale, yet the high-stakes nature of legal work demands a level of rigor that current AI systems do not provide. The central problem is not simply that LLMs hallucinate facts and references; it is that they systematically draw inferences that go beyond what the source text actually supports, presenting assumption-laden conclusions as if they were logically grounded. This proposal presents a neuro-symbolic approach to legal AI that combines the expressive power of large language models with the rigor of formal verification, aiming to make AI-assisted legal reasoning both capable and trustworthy, thus reducing the burden of manual verification without sacrificing the accountability that legal practice demands.

</details>


### [188] [SPIN: Structural LLM Planning via Iterative Navigation for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14051)
*Yusuke Ozaki,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPIN是一个结合验证DAG规划与基于前缀的执行控制的规划包装器，通过严格的有向无环图契约和增量前缀评估，在减少不必要任务执行和工具调用的同时提高了任务完成率和规划质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业LLM智能体系统通常将规划与执行分离，但LLM规划器频繁产生结构无效或不必要冗长的工作流，导致脆弱的失败以及可避免的工具和API成本。

Method: SPIN采用两个核心组件：1) 通过_validate_plan_text和修复提示(prompting)强制执行严格的DAG契约，确保生成可执行计划后再进行下游执行；2) 增量评估DAG前缀，在当此前缀足以回答查询时提前停止执行。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench的261个场景中，执行任务从1061减少到623，Accomplished从0.638提升到0.706，每次运行的工具调用从11.81降至6.82。在MCP Bench上，对GPT OSS1和Llama 4 Maverick均提升了规划、接地和依赖相关分数。

Conclusion: SPIN通过验证DAG规划和前缀级执行控制，有效解决了LLM规划器的结构无效和冗长问题，在减少执行任务和工具调用的同时提升了任务完成率，验证了该包装器在多模型上的通用有效性。

Abstract: Industrial LLM agent systems often separate planning from execution, yet LLM planners frequently produce structurally invalid or unnecessarily long workflows, leading to brittle failures and avoidable tool and API cost. We propose \texttt{SPIN}, a planning wrapper that combines validated Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) planning with prefix based execution control. \texttt{SPIN} enforces a strict DAG contract through \texttt{\_validate\_plan\_text} and repair prompting, producing executable plans before downstream execution, and then evaluates DAG prefixes incrementally to stop when the current prefix is sufficient to answer the query. On AssetOpsBench, across 261 scenarios, \texttt{SPIN} reduces executed tasks from 1061 to 623 and improves \emph{Accomplished} from 0.638 to 0.706, while reducing tool calls from 11.81 to 6.82 per run. On MCP Bench, the same wrapper improves planning, grounding, and dependency related scores for both GPT OSS1 and Llama 4 Maverick.

</details>


### [189] [MathAtlas: A Benchmark for Autoformalization in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14061)
*Nilay Patel,Noah Arias,Davit Babayan,Victoria Cochran,Timothy Libman,Hafsah Mahmood,Liam McCarty,Soli Munoz,Laurel Willey,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了MathAtlas，首个大规模研究生级别数学自动形式化基准，包含约5.2万个定理/定义/习题/示例/证明和17.8万条数学依赖关系。实验表明该基准质量高但极具挑战性，最强基线在定理陈述上仅达9.8%正确率，在深度依赖子集上仅达2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化基准主要集中在奥林匹克或本科数学，研究生和研究级数学尚未被充分探索，缺乏大规模、高难度、包含依赖关系的自动形式化基准。

Method: 从103本研究生数学教材中提取约5.2万个定理、定义、习题、示例和证明，构建包含约17.8万条关系的数学依赖图，并划分出MA-Hard子集（700个最深依赖树的实体）以评估依赖深度对性能的影响。

Result: 强基线在定理陈述上最多达9.8%正确率，定义上达16.7%；在MA-Hard子集上，最佳模型仅达2.6%正确率；依赖深度越大，性能下降越显著。

Conclusion: MathAtlas是高质量但极具挑战性的研究生级数学自动形式化基准，现有SOTA模型性能有限，尤其在处理深度依赖关系时表现不佳，该基准将促进依赖感知的自动形式化系统的发展。

Abstract: Current autoformalization benchmarks are largely focused on olympiad or undergraduate mathematics, while graduate and research-level mathematics remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce MathAtlas, the first large-scale autoformalization benchmark of in the wild graduate-level mathematics, containing ~52k theorems, definitions, exercises, examples, and proofs extracted from 103 graduate mathematics textbooks. MathAtlas is enriched with a mathematical dependency graph containing ~178k relations, and is the first autoformalization benchmark to include such relations, facilitating evaluation and development of dependency-aware autoformalization systems. Our extensive experiments show that MathAtlas is high quality but extremely challenging: strong baselines achieve at most 9.8% correctness on theorem statements and 16.7% on definitions. Furthermore, we find performance of state-of-the-art models degrades substantially with dependency depth: on MA-Hard, a subset of 700 entities with the deepest dependency trees, the best model achieves only 2.6% correctness for autoformalization on this challenging dataset. We release MathAtlas to the community as a benchmark set for large-scale autoformalization of graduate-level mathematics in the wild.

</details>


### [190] [Know When To Fold 'Em: Token-Efficient LLM Synthetic Data Generation via Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14062)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多阶段飞行拒绝(MSIFR)框架，在LLM合成数据生成过程中通过中间检查点提前终止低质量样本，减少令牌浪费，可节省11%-77%的令牌消耗且不影响评估精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM合成数据生成方法通常先完整生成输出再应用质量过滤器，导致大量被丢弃样本的令牌浪费，亟需一种能在生成过程中提前终止低质量轨迹的轻量级方案。

Method: 将生成过程分解为多个连续阶段，在每个阶段应用基于规则的快速验证器检测算术不一致、幻觉模式和格式违规等问题，实现早期拒绝；将飞行拒绝形式化为序列决策过程，证明任何非平凡丢弃策略都能减少期望令牌消耗，并证明条件效用估计构成鞅，确保早期拒绝不会偏倚保留样本的期望效用。

Result: 在5个指令微调模型和7个推理基准上，MSIFR作为独立方法可减少11%-77%的令牌消耗，与早期退出方法结合时可节省高达78.2%，同时保持或提升评估精度。

Conclusion: MSIFR为LLM合成数据生成提供了一种无需额外训练或架构修改的高效机制，证明了飞行拒绝在实用场景中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: While synthetic data generation with large language models (LLMs) is widely used in post-training pipelines, existing approaches typically generate full outputs before applying quality filters, leading to substantial token waste on samples that are ultimately discarded. To address this, we propose Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection (MSIFR), a lightweight, training-free framework that detects and terminates low-quality generation trajectories at intermediate checkpoints before they reach full completion. MSIFR decomposes the generation process into sequential stages and applies fast rule-based validators to identify arithmetic inconsistencies, hallucination patterns, and formatting violations, enabling early rejection of faulty samples. We formalize in-flight rejection as a sequential decision process and show that any non-trivial discard policy reduces expected token consumption, with stage-wise savings increasing when rejection occurs earlier in the generation pipeline. We further demonstrate that conditional utility estimates form a martingale, ensuring that early, in-flight rejection does not bias the expected utility of retained samples. Across five instruction-tuned models and seven reasoning benchmarks, MSIFR reduces token consumption by 11%-77% as a standalone method, and up to 78.2% when combined with early-exit methods, while preserving or improving evaluation accuracy. These results confirm that MSIFR provides a practical mechanism for improving the efficiency of LLM-based synthetic data generation without additional training or architectural changes.

</details>


### [191] [ClawForge: Generating Executable Interactive Benchmarks for Command-Line Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14133)
*Yuxiang Lai,Peng Xia,Haonian Ji,Kaiwen Xiong,Kaide Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Fang Wu,Jike Zhong,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClawForge是一个基于生成器的基准测试框架，用于评估智能体在存在状态冲突的可执行命令行工作流中的表现。框架通过场景模板、初始化状态、参考轨迹和验证器生成可复现的任务规范，并基于最终状态和可观察副作用进行评估。在17个场景、6个能力类别的ClawForge-Bench上，最佳模型严格准确率仅45.3%，错误状态替换率低于17%，模型间最大差异（17%-90%）取决于智能体是否在执行前检查现有状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式基准测试存在两难：手动构建任务成本高且难以扩展，静态提示评估无法捕捉智能体在持久状态上操作时出现的失败。此外，多数基准测试从干净状态初始化任务，未系统测试智能体如何处理预先存在的部分、过时或冲突的工件。

Method: 提出ClawForge框架，包含：(1) 场景模板与接地槽位的编译机制；(2) 初始化状态、参考轨迹和验证器的生成；(3) 基于归一化最终状态和可观察副作用的逐步评估，而非精确轨迹匹配。实例化为ClawForge-Bench，涵盖17个场景、6个能力类别。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，最佳模型严格准确率仅45.3%；所有模型的错误状态替换率低于17%；模型间最大分离度（17%-90%）由智能体是否在执行前检查现有状态驱动；部分模型出现近失败闭合而非早期崩溃，不同模型在状态冲突下表现出定性不同的失败风格。

Conclusion: 当前模型在处理状态冲突的交互式工作流中表现有限，尤其是缺乏主动检查现有状态的能力。ClawForge为系统评估此类场景提供了可扩展的框架，揭示了模型在状态冲突下的不同失败模式。

Abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks face a tension between scalable construction and realistic workflow evaluation. Hand-authored tasks are expensive to extend and revise, while static prompt evaluation misses failures that only appear when agents operate over persistent state. Existing interactive benchmarks have advanced agent evaluation significantly, but most initialize tasks from clean state and do not systematically test how agents handle pre-existing partial, stale, or conflicting artifacts. We present \textbf{ClawForge}, a generator-backed benchmark framework for executable command-line workflows under state conflict. The framework compiles scenario templates, grounded slots, initialized state, reference trajectories, and validators into reproducible task specifications, and evaluates agents step by step over persistent workflow surfaces using normalized end state and observable side effects rather than exact trajectory matching. We instantiate this framework as the ClawForge-Bench (17 scenarios, 6 ability categories). Results across seven frontier models show that the best model reaches only 45.3% strict accuracy, wrong-state replacement remains below 17\% for all models, and the widest model separation (17% to 90%) is driven by whether agents inspect existing state before acting. Partial-credit and step-efficiency analyses further reveal that many failures are near-miss closures rather than early breakdowns, and that models exhibit qualitatively different failure styles under state conflict.

</details>


### [192] [Distribution-Aware Algorithm Design with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14141)
*Saharsh Koganti,Priyadarsi Mishra,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究从样本中学习可执行求解器代码（而非预测器），提出“求解器提示”抽象概念，证明从固定库中学习最快样本一致求解器在正确性和运行时间上的泛化性，并在21个组合优化分布上验证，合成求解器在质量上达到0.971，速度快336.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器学习关注预测准确性，但在求解器学习中，两个求解器可能都返回正确解但运行时间差异巨大。因此需要一种不仅能保证正确性、还能优化运行时效率的学习框架。

Method: 提出“求解器提示”抽象：从样本中推断可复用结构并编译为专用求解器代码。理论证明从固定库中选择经验最快样本一致求解器在正确性和运行时间上均能泛化，且统计可识别的提示可通过多项式样本恢复和编译。实证采用LLM代码智能体在21个组合优化分布上实现该框架。

Result: 合成求解器平均归一化质量0.971，比平均启发式池提升+0.224，比最优质量启发式提升+0.098；运行速度比质量最优启发式快336.9倍，比Gurobi快342.8倍，比选定的时间限制精确后端快16.1倍。在PACE 2025 Dominating Set私密实例上，所有100个图均有效，速度比顶级竞赛求解器快约两个数量级，质量差距适中。

Conclusion: 通过将环境中的指数搜索或通用优化替换为基于分布编译的专用计算，可以显著提升求解效率，验证了学习求解器代码而非预测器的可行性。

Abstract: We study learning when the learned object is executable solver code rather than a predictor. In this setting, correctness is not enough: two solvers may both return valid solutions on the deployment distribution while differing substantially in runtime. Given samples from an unknown task distribution, the learner returns code evaluated on fresh instances by both solution quality and execution time. Our central abstraction is a \emph{solver hint}: reusable structure inferred from samples and compiled into specialized solver code. We prove that the empirically fastest sample-consistent solver from a fixed library generalizes in both correctness and runtime, and that statistically identifiable hints can be recovered and compiled from polynomially many samples.
  Empirically, we instantiate the framework with LLM code agents on \(21\) structured combinatorial-optimization target distributions across seven problem classes. The synthesized solvers reach mean normalized quality \(0.971\), improve by \(+0.224\) over the average heuristic pool and by \(+0.098\) over the highest-quality heuristic, and are \(336.9\times\), \(342.8\times\), and \(16.1\times\) faster than the quality-best heuristic, Gurobi, and the selected time-limited exact backend, respectively. On released PACE 2025 Dominating Set private instances, the synthesized solver is valid on all \(100\) graphs and runs about two orders of magnitude faster than top competition solvers, with a moderate quality gap. Inspection shows that many gains come from changing the computational scale: replacing ambient exponential search or general-purpose optimization with compiled distribution-specific computation.

</details>


### [193] [Agentic Systems as Boosting Weak Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14163)
*Varun Sunkaraneni,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Riccardo Neumarker,Tomaso Poggio,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过验证器支持的委员会搜索（verifier-backed committee search）来提升弱推理模型的能力。理论证明，重复采样可放大覆盖范围，但需要局部可验证信号（如执行、测试）才能实现可靠放大。实验表明，使用GPT-5.4 nano模型，通过批评者-比较者协调机制（k=8）达到76.4%的准确率，匹配Gemini 3 Pro和Claude Opus 4.5 Thinking的独立性能，接近79.0%的oracle上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以利用弱推理模型的委员会集成达到强模型的性能，降低对大规模强模型的依赖。

Method: 提出验证器支持的委员会搜索框架，分离出提案覆盖（coverage）、局部可识别性（local identifiability）、进展（progress）和多样性（diversity）四个维度。理论证明覆盖可通过重复采样放大，但需要局部可验证信号（如执行、测试）才能实现可靠放大。实验采用批评者-比较者协调机制（critic-comparator orchestration）。
